  "new_string": "/// Expand `~` (home) and `$VAR` / `${VAR}` (env) substitutions in a\n/// user-supplied string. Bare `~` and `~/…` are expanded via `HOME`;\n/// unset env vars are a fatal error (silent empty substitution\n/// masks typos). A bare `$` with no valid identifier after it stays\n/// literal.\npub(crate) fn expand_vars(s: &str) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let home_expanded = if s == \"~\" {\n        std::env::var(\"HOME\").map_err(|_| \"HOME is not set; cannot expand '~'\")?\n    } else if let Some(rest) = s.strip_prefix(\"~/\") {\n        let home = std::env::var(\"HOME\").map_err(|_| \"HOME is not set; cannot expand '~/'\")?;\n        format!(\"{home}/{rest}\")\n    } else {\n        s.to_string()\n    };\n\n    let mut out = String::with_capacity(home_expanded.len());\n    let mut chars = home_expanded.chars().peekable();\n    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {\n        if c != '$' {\n            out.push(c);\n            continue;\n        }\n        let (name, braced) = if chars.peek() == Some(&'{') {\n            chars.next(); // OK: peeked '{' above, next() must yield it\n            let mut name = String::new();\n            let mut closed = false;\n            for c2 in chars.by_ref() {\n                if c2 == '}' {\n                    closed = true;\n                    break;\n                }\n                name.push(c2);\n            }\n            if !closed {\n                return Err(format!(\"unterminated '${{…}}' in '{s}'\").into());\n            }\n            (name, true)\n        } else {\n            let mut name = String::new();\n            while let Some(&c2) = chars.peek() {\n                if c2.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c2 == '_' {\n                    name.push(c2);\n                    chars.next();\n                } else {\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n            (name, false)\n        };\n        if name.is_empty() {\n            out.push('$');\n            if braced {\n                out.push_str(\"{}\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n        let val =\n            std::env::var(&name).map_err(|_| format!(\"env var '${name}' is not set (in '{s}')\"))?;\n        out.push_str(&val);\n    }\n    Ok(out)\n}\n\n/// True when `s` looks like a git remote URL rather than a\n/// filesystem path. Covers scheme-qualified (`scheme://…`) and the\n/// scp-like SSH form (`user@host:path`). Everything else is a path.\npub(crate) fn is_remote_url(s: &str) -> bool {\n    if s.contains(\"://\") {\n        return true;\n    }\n    if let Some(at) = s.find('@')\n        && let Some(colon) = s.find(':')\n        && at < colon\n        && !s[..at].contains('/')\n    {\n        return true;\n    }\n    false\n}\n\n/// True when `s` is a GitHub URL. Detected by host — covers the\n/// three forms users type: SSH scp-like, `ssh://`-prefixed, and\n/// `https://` (also `http://`). GitHub URLs go through the\n/// `gh repo create` provisioner.\npub(crate) fn is_github_url(s: &str) -> bool {\n    s.starts_with(\"git@github.com:\")\n        || s.starts_with(\"ssh://git@github.com/\")\n        || s.starts_with(\"https://github.com/\")\n        || s.starts_with(\"http://github.com/\")\n}\n\n/// Normalize a `--repo-remote` value. Expand variables, then for\n/// paths make absolute; URLs pass through after expansion.\npub(crate) fn normalize_remote(s: &str) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let s = s.trim();\n    if s.is_empty() {\n        return Err(\"--repo-remote: value is empty\".into());\n    }\n    let expanded = expand_vars(s)?;\n    if is_remote_url(&expanded) {\n        return Ok(expanded);\n    }\n    let p = PathBuf::from(&expanded);\n    if p.is_absolute() {\n        Ok(expanded)\n    } else {\n        let cwd = std::env::current_dir()?;\n        Ok(cwd.join(p).to_string_lossy().to_string())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Normalize a `--repo-local` value: expand variables, make\n/// absolute. Parent doesn't need to exist yet — init creates it.\npub(crate) fn normalize_local_parent(s: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let s = s.trim();\n    if s.is_empty() {\n        return Err(\"--repo-local: value is empty\".into());\n    }\n    let expanded = expand_vars(s)?;\n    let p = PathBuf::from(&expanded);\n    if p.is_absolute() {\n        Ok(p)\n    } else {\n        let cwd = std::env::current_dir()?;\n        Ok(cwd.join(p))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Append `.git` to a URL-or-path stem if it isn't already there.\n/// Used so `--repo-remote git@github.com:u/tf1` normalizes to the\n/// same URL as the default-mode derivation.\npub(crate) fn ensure_git_suffix(s: &str) -> String {\n    if s.ends_with(\".git\") {\n        s.to_string()\n    } else {\n        format!(\"{s}.git\")\n    }\n}\n\n/// Derive the session-side URL from the code-side URL by inserting\n/// `.claude` before the trailing `.git`. Assumes the code URL has\n/// the `.git` suffix (caller ensures via `ensure_git_suffix`); if\n/// it doesn't, appends `.claude` instead. Mirrors the existing\n/// GitHub naming convention (`foo.git` ↔ `foo.claude.git`).\npub(crate) fn derive_session_url(code_url: &str) -> String {\n    match code_url.strip_suffix(\".git\") {\n        Some(stem) => format!(\"{stem}.claude.git\"),\n        None => format!(\"{code_url}.claude\"),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Derive the project NAME from a URL: last path segment, trailing\n/// `.git` stripped. Path segment boundary is the rightmost `/` or\n/// `:` (SSH scp-like). Used when NAME isn't passed positionally.\npub(crate) fn derive_name_from_url(url: &str) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let stem = url.strip_suffix(\".git\").unwrap_or(url);\n    let last = stem.rsplit(['/', ':']).next().unwrap_or(\"\"); // OK: rsplit always yields at least one element\n    if last.is_empty() {\n        return Err(format!(\"cannot derive project name from URL '{url}'\").into());\n    }\n    Ok(last.to_string())\n}\n\n/// How init should provision the remote repositories it pushes to.\n/// See `notes/chores-06.md > Generalize --scope across commands\n/// (0.40.0) > 0.40.0-1` for the dispatch rules.\n#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub(crate) enum Provisioner {\n    /// `git init --bare` on local paths under `--repo-local`.\n    LocalBareInit,\n    /// `gh repo create` for GitHub URLs (default mode or\n    /// `--repo-remote <github-url>`).\n    GhCreate,\n    /// Skip any create step; the caller pre-created the remote\n    /// (non-GitHub URLs under `--repo-remote`).\n    ExternalPreExisting,\n}\n\n/// Fully-resolved inputs to the init execution phase. Everything\n/// downstream operates on an `InitPlan` — the dispatch and\n/// ambiguity rules are localized to `plan_init` so the execution\n/// flow stays linear.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub(crate) struct InitPlan {\n    pub project_dir: PathBuf,\n    pub session_dir: PathBuf,\n    pub name: String,\n    pub session_name: String,\n    pub code_url: String,\n    pub session_url: String,\n    pub provisioner: Provisioner,\n    /// Set only when `provisioner == LocalBareInit`.\n    pub code_bare_path: Option<PathBuf>,\n    /// Set only when `provisioner == LocalBareInit`.\n    pub session_bare_path: Option<PathBuf>,\n    /// GitHub `owner/name` for the code side; only populated for\n    /// the `GhCreate` path (`gh repo create` needs it).\n    pub gh_code_slug: Option<String>,\n    /// GitHub `owner/name` for the session side; same shape.\n    pub gh_session_slug: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// Build an `InitPlan` from CLI args: validate mutual-exclusion,\n/// resolve URLs and paths, derive NAME if necessary, and pick the\n/// provisioner. Returns `Err` for any ambiguous or incomplete flag\n/// combination.\npub(crate) fn plan_init(args: &InitArgs) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    // --- Mutual-exclusion gates ---\n    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.repo_remote.is_some() {\n        return Err(\"--repo-local and --repo-remote are mutually exclusive\".into());\n    }\n    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.dir.is_some() {\n        return Err(\n            \"--repo-local and --dir are mutually exclusive (--repo-local is the parent)\".into(),\n        );\n    }\n    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.owner.is_some() {\n        return Err(\n            \"--repo-local and --owner are mutually exclusive (fixture mode has no GitHub)\".into(),\n        );\n    }\n    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.private {\n        return Err(\n            \"--repo-local and --private are mutually exclusive (fixture mode has no GitHub)\".into(),\n        );\n    }\n    if args.repo_remote.is_some() && args.owner.is_some() {\n        return Err(\n            \"--repo-remote and --owner are mutually exclusive (URL already encodes owner)\".into(),\n        );\n    }\n    if args.repo_remote.is_some() && args.private {\n        return Err(\n            \"--repo-remote and --private are mutually exclusive (visibility is set at repo creation, not here)\"\n                .into(),\n        );\n    }\n\n    match (&args.repo_local, &args.repo_remote) {\n        (Some(parent_spec), None) => plan_local_fixture(args, parent_spec),\n        (None, Some(url_spec)) => plan_external_remote(args, url_spec),\n        (None, None) => plan_default_github(args),\n        #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n        (Some(_), Some(_)) => {\n            // OK: mutual-exclusion gate above already errored on this case\n            unreachable!(\"mutual-exclusion gate earlier in plan_init\")\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Plan for `--repo-local PARENT`: fixture mode with bare repos\n/// under the same parent. Creates bare-repo paths alongside the\n/// project; none touch the network.\nfn plan_local_fixture(\n    args: &InitArgs,\n    parent_spec: &str,\n) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let name = args\n        .name\n        .clone()\n        .ok_or(\"NAME is required with --repo-local\")?;\n    let parent = normalize_local_parent(parent_spec)?;\n    let project_dir = parent.join(&name);\n    let session_dir = project_dir.join(\".claude\");\n    let code_bare = parent.join(\"remote-code.git\");\n    let session_bare = parent.join(\"remote-claude.git\");\n    let session_name = format!(\"{name}.claude\");\n    Ok(InitPlan {\n        code_url: code_bare.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        session_url: session_bare.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        code_bare_path: Some(code_bare),\n        session_bare_path: Some(session_bare),\n        project_dir,\n        session_dir,\n        name,\n        session_name,\n        provisioner: Provisioner::LocalBareInit,\n        gh_code_slug: None,\n        gh_session_slug: None,\n    })\n}\n\n/// Plan for `--repo-remote URL`: URL replaces the default\n/// derivation. Session URL derived; NAME derived from URL unless\n/// given positionally (if both, they must agree). Provisioner is\n/// `GhCreate` for GitHub URLs, `ExternalPreExisting` otherwise.\nfn plan_external_remote(\n    args: &InitArgs,\n    url_spec: &str,\n) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let normalized = normalize_remote(url_spec)?;\n    let code_url = ensure_git_suffix(&normalized);\n    let session_url = derive_session_url(&code_url);\n    let url_name = derive_name_from_url(&code_url)?;\n\n    let name = match &args.name {\n        Some(n) if *n == url_name => n.clone(),\n        Some(n) => {\n            return Err(format!(\n                \"NAME '{n}' disagrees with --repo-remote URL '{url_spec}' (derived '{url_name}')\"\n            )\n            .into());\n        }\n        None => url_name.clone(),\n    };\n    let session_name = format!(\"{name}.claude\");\n\n    let parent_dir = match &args.dir {\n        Some(d) => d.clone(),\n        None => std::env::current_dir()?,\n    };\n    let project_dir = parent_dir.join(&name);\n    let session_dir = project_dir.join(\".claude\");\n\n    let provisioner = if is_github_url(&code_url) {\n        Provisioner::GhCreate\n    } else {\n        Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting\n    };\n\n    let (gh_code_slug, gh_session_slug) = if provisioner == Provisioner::GhCreate {\n        (\n            Some(github_slug_from_url(&code_url)?),\n            Some(github_slug_from_url(&session_url)?),\n        )\n    } else {\n        (None, None)\n    };\n\n    Ok(InitPlan {\n        project_dir,\n        session_dir,\n        name,\n        session_name,\n        code_url,\n        session_url,\n        provisioner,\n        code_bare_path: None,\n        session_bare_path: None,\n        gh_code_slug,\n        gh_session_slug,\n    })\n}\n\n/// Plan for the default mode (no `--repo-local` / `--repo-remote`).\n/// NAME is required; `owner` derived via `gh whoami` unless\n/// supplied. Provisioner is always `GhCreate`.\nfn plan_default_github(args: &InitArgs) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let name = args\n        .name\n        .clone()\n        .ok_or(\"NAME is required (or pass --repo-remote URL)\")?;\n    let session_name = format!(\"{name}.claude\");\n\n    let owner = match &args.owner {\n        Some(o) => o.clone(),\n        None => gh_whoami()?,\n    };\n\n    let code_url = format!(\"git@github.com:{owner}/{name}.git\");\n    let session_url = format!(\"git@github.com:{owner}/{session_name}.git\");\n    let gh_code_slug = format!(\"{owner}/{name}\");\n    let gh_session_slug = format!(\"{owner}/{session_name}\");\n\n    let parent_dir = match &args.dir {\n        Some(d) => d.clone(),\n        None => std::env::current_dir()?,\n    };\n    let project_dir = parent_dir.join(&name);\n    let session_dir = project_dir.join(\".claude\");\n\n    Ok(InitPlan {\n        project_dir,\n        session_dir,\n        name,\n        session_name,\n        code_url,\n        session_url,\n        provisioner: Provisioner::GhCreate,\n        code_bare_path: None,\n        session_bare_path: None,\n        gh_code_slug: Some(gh_code_slug),\n        gh_session_slug: Some(gh_session_slug),\n    })\n}\n\n/// Extract the `owner/name` slug from a GitHub URL (any of the\n/// supported forms: SSH scp-like, `ssh://`, `https://`).\nfn github_slug_from_url(url: &str) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let stem = url.strip_suffix(\".git\").unwrap_or(url);\n    // The slug is the last two path components, `owner/name`.\n    if let Some(path) = stem\n        .strip_prefix(\"git@github.com:\")\n        .or_else(|| stem.strip_prefix(\"ssh://git@github.com/\"))\n        .or_else(|| stem.strip_prefix(\"https://github.com/\"))\n        .or_else(|| stem.strip_prefix(\"http://github.com/\"))\n    {\n        let parts: Vec<&str> = path.split('/').collect();\n        if parts.len() >= 2 {\n            return Ok(format!(\n                \"{}/{}\",\n                parts[parts.len() - 2],\n                parts[parts.len() - 1]\n            ));\n        }\n    }\n    Err(format!(\"cannot extract owner/name slug from GitHub URL '{url}'\").into())\n}\n\npub fn init(args: &InitArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {"
}

&symlink_dir)?;\n    sl.create(false)?;\n\n    info!(\"\");\n    info!(\"Done! Project created at {}\", project_dir.display());\n    info!(\"  Code repo:    {code_repo}  (chid={code_chid_final})\");\n    info!(\"  Session repo: {session_repo}  (chid={session_chid_final})\");\n    info!(\n        \"  Symlink:      {} -> {}\",\n        sl.symlink_path.display(),\n        sl.abs_target.display()\n    );\n\n    debug!(\"init: exit\");\n    Ok(())\n}",
  "new_string": "pub fn init(args: &InitArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"init: enter\");\n\n    let plan = plan_init(args)?;\n\n    // --- Preflight ---\n    info!(\"Preflight checks...\");\n\n    debug!(\"verify jj is installed\");\n    run(\"jj\", &[\"--version\"], Path::new(\".\")).map_err(|_| \"jj is not installed\")?;\n\n    if plan.provisioner == Provisioner::GhCreate {\n        debug!(\"verify gh CLI is installed + authenticated\");\n        run(\"gh\", &[\"auth\", \"status\"], Path::new(\".\"))\n            .map_err(|_| \"gh is not installed or not authenticated (run: gh auth login)\")?;\n    }\n\n    if plan.project_dir.exists() {\n        return Err(format!(\"'{}' already exists\", plan.project_dir.display()).into());\n    }\n\n    match &plan.provisioner {\n        Provisioner::GhCreate => {\n            // Safe to unwrap: GhCreate always populates these slugs.\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            let code_slug = plan.gh_code_slug.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: GhCreate path always sets gh_code_slug\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            let session_slug = plan.gh_session_slug.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: GhCreate path always sets gh_session_slug\n            let (code_owner, code_name) = split_slug(code_slug)?;\n            let (session_owner, session_name) = split_slug(session_slug)?;\n            if gh_repo_exists(code_owner, code_name)? {\n                return Err(format!(\"GitHub repo '{code_slug}' already exists\").into());\n            }\n            if gh_repo_exists(session_owner, session_name)? {\n                return Err(format!(\"GitHub repo '{session_slug}' already exists\").into());\n            }\n        }\n        Provisioner::LocalBareInit => {\n            // Safe to unwrap: LocalBareInit always populates these.\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            let code_bare = plan.code_bare_path.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: LocalBareInit path always sets code_bare_path\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            let session_bare = plan.session_bare_path.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: LocalBareInit path always sets session_bare_path\n            if code_bare.exists() {\n                return Err(format!(\n                    \"bare repo '{}' already exists; refusing to clobber\",\n                    code_bare.display()\n                )\n                .into());\n            }\n            if session_bare.exists() {\n                return Err(format!(\n                    \"bare repo '{}' already exists; refusing to clobber\",\n                    session_bare.display()\n                )\n                .into());\n            }\n        }\n        Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting => {\n            // For path-shaped URLs: the path must exist (caller\n            // pre-created with `git init --bare`). For scheme-URL\n            // values we can't preflight cheaply — let git push\n            // surface failures.\n            for (label, url) in [(\"code\", &plan.code_url), (\"session\", &plan.session_url)] {\n                if !is_remote_url(url) {\n                    let p = PathBuf::from(url);\n                    if !p.exists() {\n                        return Err(format!(\n                            \"--repo-remote: {label} path '{}' does not exist — \\\n                             pre-create with `git init --bare`, or use --repo-local for fixture creation\",\n                            p.display()\n                        )\n                        .into());\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let templates = match &args.use_template {\n        Some(s) => {\n            let (code_t, bot_t) = parse_use_template(s)?;\n            validate_templates(&code_t, &bot_t)?;\n            Some((code_t, bot_t))\n        }\n        None => None,\n    };\n\n    let visibility = if args.private {\n        \"--private\"\n    } else {\n        \"--public\"\n    };\n\n    if args.dry_run {\n        info!(\"Dry run — would execute:\");\n        info!(\"  1. Create directories: {}\", plan.project_dir.display());\n        info!(\"  2. git init + jj git init --colocate on both repos\");\n        info!(\"  3. Write .vc-config.toml and .gitignore to both\");\n        match &templates {\n            Some((c, b)) => {\n                info!(\n                    \"  4. Copy templates (non-hidden) into both repos + rewrite README.md first line\"\n                );\n                info!(\"       code: {}\", c.display());\n                info!(\"       bot:  {}\", b.display());\n            }\n            None => info!(\"  4. (skipped — no --use-template)\"),\n        }\n        info!(\"  5. jj commit both with placeholder ochids\");\n        info!(\"  6. Get both chids, jj describe both with correct ochids\");\n        info!(\"  7. Remove jj from both (git clean -xdf)\");\n        match &plan.provisioner {\n            Provisioner::GhCreate => {\n                info!(\n                    \"  8. gh repo create {} {visibility}; push to {}\",\n                    plan.gh_session_slug.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\"), // OK: GhCreate always sets this (dry-run display only)\n                    plan.session_url\n                );\n                info!(\n                    \"  9. gh repo create {} {visibility}; push to {}\",\n                    plan.gh_code_slug.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\"), // OK: GhCreate always sets this (dry-run display only)\n                    plan.code_url\n                );\n            }\n            Provisioner::LocalBareInit => {\n                info!(\n                    \"  8. git init --bare {}; push to {}\",\n                    plan.session_bare_path\n                        .as_ref()\n                        .map(|p| p.display().to_string())\n                        .unwrap_or_default(), // OK: LocalBareInit always sets this (dry-run display only)\n                    plan.session_url\n                );\n                info!(\n                    \"  9. git init --bare {}; push to {}\",\n                    plan.code_bare_path\n                        .as_ref()\n                        .map(|p| p.display().to_string())\n                        .unwrap_or_default(), // OK: LocalBareInit always sets this (dry-run display only)\n                    plan.code_url\n                );\n            }\n            Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting => {\n                info!(\n                    \"  8. skip create; push to pre-existing {}\",\n                    plan.session_url\n                );\n                info!(\"  9. skip create; push to pre-existing {}\", plan.code_url);\n            }\n        }\n        info!(\"  10. jj git init --colocate on both repos\");\n        info!(\"  11. Create Claude Code symlink\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    // Step 1: Create directories. For LocalBareInit also create the\n    // parent so the bare-repo init (step 8/9) has a home.\n    info!(\"Step 1: Creating project directories...\");\n    if let Some(parent) = plan.project_dir.parent() {\n        mkdir_p(parent)?;\n    }\n    mkdir_p(&plan.project_dir)?;\n    mkdir_p(&plan.session_dir)?;\n\n    // Step 2: git init + jj init on both repos\n    info!(\"Step 2: Initializing repos...\");\n    debug!(\"seed project_dir with an empty git repo\");\n    run(\"git\", &[\"init\"], &plan.project_dir)?;\n    debug!(\"colocate jj atop the git repo so jj + git share .git state\");\n    run(\"jj\", &[\"git\", \"init\", \"--colocate\"], &plan.project_dir)?;\n    debug!(\"seed session_dir with an empty git repo\");\n    run(\"git\", &[\"init\"], &plan.session_dir)?;\n    debug!(\"colocate jj atop the session git repo\");\n    run(\"jj\", &[\"git\", \"init\", \"--colocate\"], &plan.session_dir)?;\n\n    // Step 3: Write config files\n    info!(\"Step 3: Writing config files...\");\n    write_file(&plan.project_dir.join(\".vc-config.toml\"), VC_CONFIG_CODE)?;\n    write_file(&plan.project_dir.join(\".gitignore\"), GITIGNORE_CODE)?;\n    write_file(&plan.session_dir.join(\".vc-config.toml\"), VC_CONFIG_SESSION)?;\n    write_file(&plan.session_dir.join(\".gitignore\"), GITIGNORE_SESSION)?;\n\n    // Step 4: Copy templates (if --use-template)\n    if let Some((code_t, bot_t)) = &templates {\n        info!(\"Step 4: Copying templates...\");\n        info!(\n            \"  code: {} -> {}\",\n            code_t.display(),\n            plan.project_dir.display()\n        );\n        copy_template_recursive(code_t, &plan.project_dir)?;\n        rewrite_readme_first_line(&plan.project_dir, &plan.name)?;\n        info!(\n            \"  bot:  {} -> {}\",\n            bot_t.display(),\n            plan.session_dir.display()\n        );\n        copy_template_recursive(bot_t, &plan.session_dir)?;\n        rewrite_readme_first_line(&plan.session_dir, &plan.session_name)?;\n    }\n\n    // Step 5: jj commit both with placeholder ochids\n    info!(\"Step 5: Committing both repos with placeholder ochids...\");\n    debug!(\"code side: initial commit with placeholder ochid (rewritten in step 6)\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"commit\", \"-m\", \"Initial commit\\n\\nochid: /none\"],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"session side: initial commit with placeholder ochid (rewritten in step 6)\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"commit\", \"-m\", \"Initial commit\\n\\nochid: /none\"],\n        &plan.session_dir,\n    )?;\n\n    // Step 6: Get both chids, then describe both with correct ochids\n    info!(\"Step 6: Setting ochid cross-references...\");\n    let code_chid = jj_chid(\"@-\", &plan.project_dir)?;\n    let session_chid = jj_chid(\"@-\", &plan.session_dir)?;\n\n    let code_desc = format!(\"Initial commit\\n\\nochid: /.claude/{session_chid}\");\n    let session_desc = format!(\"Initial commit\\n\\nochid: /{code_chid}\");\n    debug!(\"code side: rewrite initial commit's ochid to point at session chid\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"describe\", \"@-\", \"-m\", &code_desc],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"session side: rewrite initial commit's ochid to point at code chid\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"describe\", \"@-\", \"-m\", &session_desc],\n        &plan.session_dir,\n    )?;\n\n    {\n        debug!(\"surface post-describe git hashes for the debug log\");\n        let hash = run(\"git\", &[\"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"], &plan.project_dir)?;\n        debug!(\"code repo: chid={code_chid} hash={hash}\");\n        let hash = run(\"git\", &[\"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"], &plan.session_dir)?;\n        debug!(\".claude:   chid={session_chid} hash={hash}\");\n    }\n\n    // Step 7: Set bookmarks (creates git branches), then remove jj\n    info!(\"Step 7: Setting bookmarks and removing jj...\");\n    debug!(\"place code-side main bookmark at the initial commit\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"bookmark\", \"set\", \"main\", \"-r\", \"@-\"],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"place session-side main bookmark at the initial commit\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"bookmark\", \"set\", \"main\", \"-r\", \"@-\"],\n        &plan.session_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"strip jj state from session side before its git push\");\n    run(\"git\", &[\"clean\", \"-xdf\"], &plan.session_dir)?;\n    debug!(\"strip jj state from code side, preserving nested .claude/\");\n    run(\n        \"git\",\n        &[\"clean\", \"-xdf\", \"--exclude\", \".claude\"],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"re-attach session HEAD to main after clean\");\n    run(\"git\", &[\"checkout\", \"main\"], &plan.session_dir)?;\n    debug!(\"re-attach code HEAD to main after clean\");\n    run(\"git\", &[\"checkout\", \"main\"], &plan.project_dir)?;\n\n    // Steps 8/9: provision the remote for each side, then push.\n    // Dispatch varies per provisioner; push retry is uniform.\n    run_remote_step(\n        \"Step 8\",\n        \"session\",\n        &plan,\n        &plan.session_url,\n        plan.gh_session_slug.as_deref(),\n        plan.session_bare_path.as_deref(),\n        visibility,\n        &plan.session_dir,\n        args,\n    )?;\n    run_remote_step(\n        \"Step 9\",\n        \"code\",\n        &plan,\n        &plan.code_url,\n        plan.gh_code_slug.as_deref(),\n        plan.code_bare_path.as_deref(),\n        visibility,\n        &plan.project_dir,\n        args,\n    )?;\n\n    // Step 10: Re-initialize jj on both repos\n    info!(\"Step 10: Re-initializing jj on both repos...\");\n    debug!(\"re-colocate jj atop the now-remote-linked code repo\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"--quiet\", \"git\", \"init\", \"--colocate\"],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"restore code-side main bookmark after re-init\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"bookmark\", \"set\", \"main\", \"-r\", \"@-\"],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"enable jj tracking of code-side main against origin\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"bookmark\", \"track\", \"main\", \"--remote=origin\"],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    crate::common::verify_tracking(&plan.project_dir, \"main\")?;\n    let code_chid_final = jj_chid(\"@-\", &plan.project_dir)?;\n\n    debug!(\"re-colocate jj atop the now-remote-linked session repo\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"--quiet\", \"git\", \"init\", \"--colocate\"],\n        &plan.session_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"restore session-side main bookmark after re-init\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"bookmark\", \"set\", \"main\", \"-r\", \"@-\"],\n        &plan.session_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"enable jj tracking of session-side main against origin\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"bookmark\", \"track\", \"main\", \"--remote=origin\"],\n        &plan.session_dir,\n    )?;\n    crate::common::verify_tracking(&plan.session_dir, \"main\")?;\n    let session_chid_final = jj_chid(\"@-\", &plan.session_dir)?;\n\n    // Step 11: Create Claude Code symlink\n    info!(\"Step 11: Creating Claude Code symlink...\");\n    let symlink_dir = {\n        let home =\n            std::env::var(\"HOME\").map_err(|_| \"HOME environment variable not set\".to_string())?;\n        PathBuf::from(home).join(\".claude\").join(\"projects\")\n    };\n\n    let sl = symlink::SymLink::new(&plan.project_dir, Path::new(\".claude\"), &symlink_dir)?;\n    sl.create(false)?;\n\n    info!(\"\");\n    info!(\"Done! Project created at {}\", plan.project_dir.display());\n    info!(\n        \"  Code repo:    {}  (chid={code_chid_final})\",\n        plan.code_url\n    );\n    info!(\n        \"  Session repo: {}  (chid={session_chid_final})\",\n        plan.session_url\n    );\n    info!(\n        \"  Symlink:      {} -> {}\",\n        sl.symlink_path.display(),\n        sl.abs_target.display()\n    );\n\n    debug!(\"init: exit\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Execute Step 8 / Step 9 for one side: provision the remote per\n/// the plan's provisioner (gh create, local bare init, or skip),\n/// set up `origin`, and push main with retry. Centralizing this\n/// keeps the two sides' step bodies identical under dispatch.\n#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]\nfn run_remote_step(\n    step_label: &str,\n    side_label: &str,\n    plan: &InitPlan,\n    remote_url: &str,\n    gh_slug: Option<&str>,\n    bare_path: Option<&Path>,\n    visibility: &str,\n    push_from: &Path,\n    args: &InitArgs,\n) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    match &plan.provisioner {\n        Provisioner::GhCreate => {\n            // Safe: GhCreate always supplies gh_slug.\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            let slug = gh_slug.unwrap(); // OK: GhCreate path always sets gh_slug\n            info!(\"{step_label}: Creating GitHub repo {slug} ({side_label})...\");\n            debug!(\"create {side_label}-side remote on GitHub\");\n            run(\"gh\", &[\"repo\", \"create\", slug, visibility], &plan.project_dir)?;\n        }\n        Provisioner::LocalBareInit => {\n            // Safe: LocalBareInit always supplies bare_path.\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            let bare = bare_path.unwrap(); // OK: LocalBareInit path always sets bare_path\n            info!(\n                \"{step_label}: Initializing local bare repo at {} ({side_label})...\",\n                bare.display()\n            );\n            debug!(\"init {side_label}-side bare repo as the local origin\");\n            run(\n                \"git\",\n                &[\"init\", \"--bare\", &bare.to_string_lossy()],\n                Path::new(\".\"),\n            )?;\n        }\n        Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting => {\n            info!(\"{step_label}: Using pre-existing {side_label} remote {remote_url}\");\n        }\n    }\n    debug!(\"point {side_label}-side git at its remote\");\n    run(\"git\", &[\"remote\", \"add\", \"origin\", remote_url], push_from)?;\n    debug!(\"publish {side_label}-side initial commit; retry for GhCreate's async propagation\");\n    run_retry(\n        \"git\",\n        &[\"push\", \"-u\", \"origin\", \"main\"],\n        push_from,\n        args.push_retries,\n        args.push_retry_delay,\n    )?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Split an `owner/name` slug. Errors if the shape is wrong (no `/`\n/// or more than one `/`).\nfn split_slug(slug: &str) -> Result<(&str, &str), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let parts: Vec<&str> = slug.split('/').collect();\n    if parts.len() != 2 || parts[0].is_empty() || parts[1].is_empty() {\n        return Err(format!(\"unexpected GitHub slug '{slug}' (want 'owner/name')\").into());\n    }\n    Ok((parts[0], parts[1]))\n}"
}

  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn defaults() {\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"init\", \"my-project\"]);\n        assert_eq!(args.name.as_deref(), Some(\"my-project\"));\n        assert!(args.owner.is_none());\n        assert!(args.dir.is_none());\n        assert!(!args.private);\n        assert!(!args.dry_run);\n        assert_eq!(args.push_retries, 5);\n        assert_eq!(args.push_retry_delay, 3);\n        assert!(args.repo_local.is_none());\n        assert!(args.repo_remote.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn all_opts() {\n        let args = parse(&[\n            \"vc-x1\",\n            \"init\",\n            \"my-project\",\n            \"--owner\",\n            \"myorg\",\n            \"--dir\",\n            \"/tmp/projects\",\n            \"--private\",\n            \"--dry-run\",\n            \"--push-retries\",\n            \"10\",\n            \"--push-retry-delay\",\n            \"5\",\n            \"--use-template\",\n            \"/tmp/tmpl,/tmp/tmpl.claude\",\n        ]);\n        assert_eq!(args.name.as_deref(), Some(\"my-project\"));\n        assert_eq!(args.owner.as_deref(), Some(\"myorg\"));\n        assert_eq!(args.dir, Some(PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/projects\")));\n        assert!(args.private);\n        assert!(args.dry_run);\n        assert_eq!(args.push_retries, 10);\n        assert_eq!(args.push_retry_delay, 5);\n        assert_eq!(\n            args.use_template.as_deref(),\n            Some(\"/tmp/tmpl,/tmp/tmpl.claude\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn name_optional_at_parse_time() {\n        // Clap accepts missing NAME; the dispatcher errors at runtime\n        // if neither NAME nor --repo-remote provides one.\n        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from([\"vc-x1\", \"init\"]);\n        assert!(cli.is_ok());\n        let InitArgs { name, .. } = match cli.unwrap().command {\n            Commands::Init(a) => a,\n            _ => panic!(\"expected Init\"),\n        };\n        assert!(name.is_none());\n    }"
}

---------- 0.40.0-1: --repo-local / --repo-remote helpers ----------\n\n    #[test]\n    fn repo_local_parses() {\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"init\", \"tf1\", \"--repo-local\",    \"init\",\n            \"tf1\",\n            \"--repo-remote\",\n            \"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1\",\n        ]);\n        assert_eq!(\n            args.repo_remote.as_deref(),\n            Some(\"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn is_remote_url_classifies() {\n        assert!(is_remote_url(\"https://host/path\"));\n        assert!(is_remote_url(\"ssh://git@host/path\"));\n        assert!(is_remote_url(\"file:///abs/path\"));\n        assert!(is_remote_url(\"git@github.com:owner/repo.git\"));\n    #[test]\n    fn is_github_url_classifies() {\n        assert!(is_github_url(\"git@github.com:owner/repo\"));\n        assert!(is_github_url(\"git@github.com:owner/repo.git\"));\n        assert!(is_github_url(\"ssh://git@github.com/owner/repo.git\"));\n        assert!(is_github_url(\"https://github.com/owner/repo\"));\n        assert!(is_github_url(\"http://github.com/owner/repo\"));\n        assert!(!is_github_url(\"git@gitlab.com:owner/repo.git\"));\n        assert!(!is_github_url(\"https://gitlab.com/owner/repo\"));\n        assert!(!is_github_url(\"/tmp/foo.git\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn ensure_git_suffix_adds_when_missing() {\n        assert_eq!(ensure_git_suffix(\"foo\"), \"foo.git\");\n        assert_eq!(ensure_git_suffix(\"foo.git\"), \"foo.git\");\n        assert_eq!(\n            ensure_git_suffix(\"git@github.com:u/p\"),\n            \"git@github.com:u/p.git\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn derive_session_url_inserts_claude_before_git() {\n        assert_eq!(\n            derive_session_url(\"git@github.com:u/p.git\"),\n            \"git@github.com:u/p.claude.git\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(derive_session_url(\"/tmp/foo.git\"), \"/tmp/foo.claude.git\");\n        // Without .git suffix, falls back to plain append.\n        assert_eq!(derive_session_url(\"/tmp/foo\"), \"/tmp/foo.claude\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn derive_name_from_url_strips_git_and_takes_last_segment() {\n        assert_eq!(\n            derive_name_from_url(\"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1\").unwrap(),\n            \"tf1\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            derive_name_from_url(\"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1.git\").unwrap(),\n            \"tf1\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            derive_name_from_url(\"https://github.com/winksaville/tf1.git\").unwrap(),\n            \"tf1\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(derive_name_from_url(\"/tmp/foo.git\").unwrap(), \"foo\");\n        assert_eq!(derive_name_from_url(\"/tmp/foo\").unwrap(), \"foo\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn expand_vars_tilde() {\n        let prev = std::env::var(\"HOME\").ok();\n        // SAFETY: env-var manipulation is genuinely racy with parallel\n        // test runners. Tests touching $VAR and $HOME live in this\n        // module; if flakiness emerges, run with --test-threads=1.\n        unsafe {\n            std::env::set_var(\"HOME\", \"/h\");\n        }\n        assert_eq!(expand_vars(\"~/foo\").unwrap(), \"/h/foo\");\n        assert_eq!(expand_vars(\"~\").unwrap(), \"/h\");\n        // SAFETY: see above.\n        unsafe {\n            match prev {\n                Some(v) => std::env::set_var(\"HOME\", v),\n                None => std::env::remove_var(\"HOME\"),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn expand_vars_envvar() {\n        let key = \"VC_X1_TEST_EXPAND_VAR\";\n        // SAFETY: see expand_vars_tilde test.\n        unsafe {\n            std::env::set_var(key, \"VAL\");\n        }\n        assert_eq!(expand_vars(\"$VC_X1_TEST_EXPAND_VAR/x\").unwrap(), \"VAL/x\");\n        assert_eq!(expand_vars(\"a${VC_X1_TEST_EXPAND_VAR}b\").unwrap(), \"aVALb\");\n        assert_eq!(expand_vars(\"$/no-name\").unwrap(), \"$/no-name\");\n        assert!(expand_vars(\"$VC_X1_TEST_DEFINITELY_UNSET_xyz\").is_err());\n        // SAFETY: see expand_vars_tilde test.\n        unsafe {\n            std::env::remove_var(key);\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn expand_vars_unterminated_brace_errors() {\n        assert!(expand_vars(\"${UNCLOSED\").is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn normalize_remote_passthrough_url() {\n        let s = \"git@github.com:u/p.git\";\n        assert_eq!(normalize_remote(s).unwrap(), s);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn normalize_remote_canonicalizes_relative_path() {\n        let cwd = std::env::current_dir().unwrap();\n        let got = normalize_remote(\"./foo.git\").unwrap();\n        assert!(got.starts_with(&cwd.to_string_lossy().to_string()));\n    }\n\n    // ---------- plan_init dispatch + ambiguity ----------\n\n    fn fixture(\n        name: Option<&str>,\n        repo_local: name.map(str::to_string),\n            owner: owner.map(str::to_string),\n            dir: dir.map(PathBuf::from),\n            private,\n            dry_run: true,\n            push_retries: 5,\n assert_eq!(plan.provisioner, Provisioner::LocalBareInit);\n        assert_eq!(plan.project_dir, PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/xyz/tf1\"));\n        assert_eq!(plan.session_dir, PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/xyz/tf1/.claude\"));\n        assert_eq!(\n            plan.code_bare_path,\n            Some(PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/xyz/remote-code.git\"))\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            plan.session_bare_path,\n            Some(PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/xyz/remote-claude.git\"))\n        );\n        assert_eq!(plan.code_url, \"/tmp/xyz/remote-code.git\");\n        assert_eq!(plan.session_url, \"/tmp/xyz/remote-claude.git\");\n        assert_eq!(plan.name, \"tf1\");\n        assert_eq!(plan.session_name, \"tf1.claude\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn plan_external_remote_github_with_name() {\n        let args = fixture(\n            Some(\"tf1\"),\n            None,\n            Some(\"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1\"),\n            None,\n            None,\n            false,\n        );\n        let plan = plan_init(&args).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(plan.provisioner, Provisioner::GhCreate);\n        assert_eq!(plan.name, \"tf1\");\n        assert_eq!(plan.code_url, \"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1.git\");\n        assert_eq!(plan.session_url, \"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1.claude.git\");\n        assert_eq!(plan.gh_code_slug.as_deref(), Some(\"winksaville/tf1\"));\n        assert_eq!(\n            plan.gh_session_slug.as_deref(),\n            Some(\"winksaville/tf1.claude\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn plan_external_remote_derives_name_from_url() {\n        let args = fixture(\n            None,\n            None,\n            Some(\"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1\"),\n plan_default_and_explicit_github_url_are_equivalent() {\n        let explicit = fixture(\n            Some(\"tf1\"),\n            None,\n            Some(\"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1\"),\n            None,\n            None,\n            false,\n        );\n        let default_with_owner = fixture(Some(\"tf1\"), None, None, None, Some(\"winksaville\"), false);\n        let p1 = plan_init(&explicit).unwrap();\n        let p2 = plan_init(&default_with_owner).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(p1.code_url, p2.code_url);\n        assert_eq!(p1.session_url, p2.session_url);\n        assert_eq!(p1.provisioner, p2.provisioner);\n        assert_eq!(p1.gh_code_slug, p2.gh_code_slug);\n        assert_eq!(p1.gh_session_slug, p2.gh_session_slug);\n    }\n\n    // ---------- Ambiguity rejections ----------\n\n    #[test]\n    fn ambig_repo_local_and_repo_remote() {\n        let args = fixture(\n            Some(\"tf1\"),\n            Some(\"/tmp/xyz\"),\n 807	## Generalize --scope across commands (0.40.0)
808	
809	Foundation for item #1 on todo ([60]): commands should support
810	single-repo, dual-repo, and plain-old-repo (POR) workspaces
811	without hard-coding the `.claude` dual-repo assumption.
812	
813	**Scope default resolution** (no `--scope` given):
814	
815	- No `.vc-config.toml` → `--scope=app`.
816	- `.vc-config.toml` present but `other-repo` missing or empty
817	  → `--scope=app`.
818	- `.vc-config.toml` with a non-empty `other-repo` → `--scope=both`.
819	
820	**Ambiguity rule.** Any ambiguous combination of scope-related
821	flags on a single invocation (e.g. `-R` with `--scope`) is a
822	fatal error — callers must pick one way to express repo
823	selection per call.
824	
825	**Cycle steps.**
826	
827	- **0.40.0-0** — this plan. Notes + version bump only.
828	- **0.40.0-1** — `vc-x1 init --scope=app|other|both`.
829	  - `app` → single-repo workspace (`.vc-config.toml` with
830	    `path = "/"` and no `other-repo`; no `.claude` subdir; one
831	    GitHub repo; no `ochid:` trailer on the initial commit).
832	  - `both` (default) → current dual-repo behavior.
833	  - `other` → error; meaningless at init time.
834	  - Every other subcommand errors loudly if `--scope` is passed.
835	- Subsequent `-N` steps wire `--scope` into sync, push,
836	  finalize, and the read-only commands. Specifics decided when
837	  each lands.
838	
839	**Helper placement is deliberately open** — `common.rs`,
840	`init.rs`, or a dedicated `scope.rs` / `scope_helpers.rs` are
841	all fine for step `-1`; we can refactor later once more call
842	sites exist. The plan is subject to change as we learn; `-0`
843	records the current best guess, not a contract.
844	
845	# References
846	
847	[57]: /notes/chores-05.md#capture-squash-mode--scope-design-for-push-0374
848	[60]: #generalize---scope-to-all-commands-design
849	[61]: #push-hardening-state--stage-sanity-design
850	[63]: #non-tracking-remote-bookmark-detection-design
851	[64]: #notes-restructure-chores-06--trim-long-todo-entries-0377
852	[65]: #scope-design-refinements-0378
853	[66]: #bookmark-tracking-verification-0380
854	[67]: #push-hardening-state--stage-sanity-0390
855	[68]: #source-code-design-ref-convention-design
856	[69]: #vc-x1-validate-repo-command-design
```

The plan is subject to change as we learn; `-0`\nrecords the current best guess, not a contract.\n\n# References",
  "new_string": "## Generalize --scope across commands (0.40.0)\n\nFoundation for item #1 on todo ([60]). Commands should support\nsingle-repo, dual-repo, and plain-old-repo (POR) workspaces\nwithout hard-coding the `.claude` dual-repo assumption.\n\n**Scope default resolution** (no `--scope` given):\n\n- No `.vc-config.toml` → `--scope=app`.\n- `.vc-config.toml` with `other-repo` missing/empty → `--scope=app`.\n- `.vc-config.toml` with a non-empty `other-repo` → `--scope=both`.\n\n**Ambiguity rule.** Any ambiguous combination of scope-related\nflags on one invocation is a fatal error — callers pick exactly\none way to express repo selection per call.\n\n**Cycle steps.**\n\n- **0.40.0-0** — this plan. Notes + version bump only.\n- **0.40.0-1** — `vc-x1 init --repo-local` + `--repo-remote`.\n  - Decouple init from `gh repo create`; unblock testable\n    fixture-mode workflows and non-GitHub remotes.\n  - **Ordering rationale (2026-04-24):** doing `--scope=app`\n    first would build on a still-GitHub-coupled init and\n    immediately need rework; flipping the order means `-2`\n    lands cleanly on a remote-agnostic flow.\n- **0.40.0-2** — `vc-x1 init --scope=app|other|both`.\n  - `app` → single-repo workspace (no `.claude` subdir; one\n    repo; no `ochid:` trailer on the initial commit).\n  - `both` (default) → current dual-repo behavior.\n  - `other` → fatal; meaningless at init time.\n  - Other subcommands error if `--scope` is passed.\n- Subsequent `-N` steps wire `--scope` into sync, push,\n  finalize, read-only commands. Specifics decided per step.\n\n**Helper placement** is deliberately open.\n\n- `common.rs`, `init.rs`, or `scope.rs` / `scope_helpers.rs`\n  are all fine; refactor later once more call sites exist.\n- Plan is subject to change; `-0` records best guess, not a\n  contract.\n\n### 0.40.0-1: init remote decoupling\n\nTwo new flags on `vc-x1 init` plus a unified\n`Provisioner` dispatch that subsumes the existing default mode.\n\n**Flags.**\n\n- `--repo-local <PARENT>` — fixture mode. Creates\n  `<PARENT>/<NAME>/`, `<PARENT>/<NAME>/.claude/`,\n  `<PARENT>/remote-code.git`, `<PARENT>/remote-claude.git`.\n  No `gh`, no network. Supports `~/…`, `$VAR`, `${VAR}`.\n- `--repo-remote <URL>` — single URL stem. Session URL\n  derived (insert `.claude` before trailing `.git`, else\n  append `.claude`). Supports paths, `file:///…`, HTTPS,\n  SSH (scp-like and `ssh://`), and `~/…`/`$VAR`.\n\n**Equivalence (winksaville case):** `vc-x1 init tf1`,\n`vc-x1 init tf1 --owner winksaville`, and\n`vc-x1 init --repo-remote git@github.com:winksaville/tf1`\nall resolve to the same plan and execute identically.\n\n**Provisioner dispatch.** All three modes feed an `InitPlan`\nwith `provisioner ∈ {LocalBareInit, GhCreate,\nExternalPreExisting}`.\n\n- `LocalBareInit` — `--repo-local`. `git init --bare` on the\n  two bare paths.\n- `GhCreate` — default mode, or `--repo-remote` with a\n  GitHub URL (host detection on the URL string). `gh repo\n  create` for both sides.\n- `ExternalPreExisting` — `--repo-remote` with a non-GitHub\n  URL/path. No create step; caller pre-creates.\n\n**Ambiguity rejections (fatal).**\n\n- `--repo-local` ⊕ `--repo-remote`.\n- `--repo-local` ⊕ `--dir` / `--owner` / `--private`.\n- `--repo-remote` ⊕ `--owner` / `--private`.\n- `NAME` and `--repo-remote` URL with disagreeing names.\n- `--repo-local` without `NAME`.\n\n**NAME derivation.** `--repo-remote` URL's last path segment\n(trailing `.git` stripped) is used when positional `NAME` is\nabsent. NAME stays optional at the clap layer; runtime\nerrors cover the missing-required cases.\n\n**Documentary `debug!` lines** — every `run()` call in `init`\nhas a `debug!(…)` immediately above it documenting intent\n(while `common::run` already logs the action). New convention\n(2026-04-24); applied to `init` here, retrofit later if useful.\n\n**Future steps unblocked.**\n\n- `-3` migrates sync/push integration tests from `test_fixture`\n  to `init --repo-local`.\n- `-4` retires `test_fixture` once no callers remain.\n\n- `Cargo.toml`: `0.40.0-0` → `0.40.0-1`.\n- `src/init.rs`: new flags, `Provisioner` enum, `InitPlan`,\n  `plan_init` dispatcher, `run_remote_step`, helpers\n  (`expand_vars`, `is_remote_url`, `is_github_url`,\n  `normalize_remote`, `normalize_local_parent`,\n  `ensure_git_suffix`, `derive_session_url`,\n  `derive_name_from_url`, `github_slug_from_url`,\n  `split_slug`), 17 new tests, documentary `debug!`\n  retrofit on every `run()`.\n- `notes/chores-06.md`: this subsection + amended cycle\n  steps (`-1` ↔ `-2` swap, ordering rationale).\n\n# References"
}
```

## Proposed CLAUDE.md refinement

The user-facing convention you've now stated twice (function docs + chore descriptions) belongs in CLAUDE.md so it survives across sessions. The existing `### Doc comments on every file, function, and method` section says "Keep them brief — one sentence of purpose is often enough" but doesn't pin down the *shape*. I'd append a paragraph there:

```markdown
**Shape:** short intro (1–3 sentences, shorter is better),
then a `-` bullet list for any structured details. Avoid
long prose paragraphs — they read as a wall of text and
hide the structure that bullets make scannable. This
applies to:

- Module/function/struct/field doc comments in `.rs` files.
- Chore descriptions in `notes/chores-NN.md`.
- Todo entries in `notes/todo.md`.
```

That'd live under "Doc comments on every file, function, and method" with a small section break, or as a new top-level subsection under `## Code Conventions`. Either placement works — the content's the load-bearing part.

OK to add to CLAUDE.md as part of the `-1` commit? (Logically tied — both shipped together.)

241	### Doc comments on every file, function, and method
242	
243	Every `.rs` file must begin with a `//!` module docstring. Every

251	inside function bodies remain discouraged unless they capture a

Create a new dual-repo project

Usage: vc-x1 init [OPTIONS] [NAME]

Arguments:
  [NAME]
          Project name (directory and GitHub repo name).
          
          - Optional with `--repo-remote` (derived from URL's last path segment,
          trailing `.git` stripped).
          
          - Required with `--repo-local` and in default mode.
          
          - Fatal if both given and they disagree.

Options:
      --owner <OWNER>
          GitHub user or organization.
          
          - Only meaningful in default mode.
          
          - Fatal with `--repo-local` or `--repo-remote`.

  -v, --verbose...
          Verbose output: -v debug, -vv trace

      --dir <DIR>
          Parent directory to create the project under [default: cwd].
          
          - Fatal with `--repo-local` (that flag supplies the parent).

      --log <LOG>
          Log file path (captures all levels)

      --private
          Create private GitHub repos (default: public).
          
          - Only meaningful in default mode.
          
          - Fatal with `--repo-local` or `--repo-remote`.

      --dry-run
          Dry run — show what would be done without executing

      --push-retries <PUSH_RETRIES>
          Max push retries after repo creation [default: 5]
          
          [default: 5]

      --push-retry-delay <PUSH_RETRY_DELAY>
          Seconds between push retries [default: 3]
          
          [default: 3]

      --use-template <CODE[,BOT]>
          Seed both repos from template directories.
          
          Value is `CODE[,BOT]`. If `BOT` is omitted, defaults to the sibling
          directory `<CODE>.claude` (file-name concat, not path join — they are
          not nested). Non-hidden contents are copied recursively; hidden
          entries (names starting with `.`) are skipped since init creates the
          repo's own hidden files. If either template has a `README.md`, its
          first line is rewritten to `# <repo-name>`.

      --repo-local <PARENT>
          Fixture mode — fully self-contained local workspace.
          
          Value is a single parent directory.
          
          - Project + session at `<PARENT>/<NAME>/` and
          `<PARENT>/<NAME>/.claude/`.
          
          - Bare origins at `<PARENT>/remote-code.git` and
          `<PARENT>/remote-claude.git`.
          
          - No `gh`, no network.
          
          - Supports `~/…`, `$VAR`, `${VAR}`; relative paths against cwd.
          
          - Exclusive with `--repo-remote`, `--dir`, `--owner`, `--private`.

      --repo-remote <URL>
          Override the `origin` URL stem (single value).
          
          - Session URL derived: insert `.claude` before `.git` suffix, or
          append if no `.git`.
          
          - GitHub URLs → `gh repo create` (same as default).
          
          - Other URLs → caller pre-creates the remote; init only pushes.
          
          - Supports `~/…`, `$VAR`, `${VAR}` in path-shaped values.
          
          - Exclusive with `--repo-local`, `--owner`, `--private`.

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

  -V, --version
          Print version

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/CLAUDE.md",
  "old_string": "**Shape:** short intro (1–3 sentences, shorter is better), then\na `-` bullet list for any structured details. Avoid long prose\nparagraphs — they read as a wall of text and hide the structure\nthat bullets make scannable. Same shape applies to:\n\n- Module / function / struct / field doc comments in `.rs` files.\n- Chore descriptions in `notes/chores-NN.md`.\n- Todo entries in `notes/todo.md` (when an item needs more than\n  one line of detail; pure one-liners are still fine).",
  "new_string": "**Shape:** short intro (1–3 sentences, shorter is better), then\na `-` bullet list for any structured details. Avoid long prose\nparagraphs — they read as a wall of text and hide the structure\nthat bullets make scannable. Same shape applies to:\n\n- Module / function / struct / field doc comments in `.rs` files.\n- Chore descriptions in `notes/chores-NN.md`.\n- Todo entries in `notes/todo.md` (when an item needs more than\n  one line of detail; pure one-liners are still fine).\n\n**Clap-derive args:** doc comments on `#[arg(...)]` fields drive\n`--help` output. Clap reflows by default and collapses bullets\ninto running prose. Add `#[arg(verbatim_doc_comment, ...)]` on\nany field whose doc comment uses bullets so each `- …` lands on\nits own line in the rendered help."
}
```

On to step `-2`: `vc-x1 init --scope=app|other|both`. Plan:

## Step `-2` design

**`src/scope.rs`** (new) — `Scope` enum (`App`, `Other`, `Both`) with clap `ValueEnum`. Lives separately so future `-3`+ steps wire it into other commands without circular imports.

**`src/main.rs`** — `mod scope;`.

**`src/init.rs`:**
- Add `scope: Option<Scope>` field on `InitArgs`. Default (None) → `Both` for backward compatibility.
- Two new constants: `VC_CONFIG_APP_ONLY` (just `path = "/"`, no `other-repo`) and `GITIGNORE_APP_ONLY` (no `/.claude` line).
- Extend `InitPlan` to express single-repo mode. Path I'm taking: make session-related fields `Option<...>` (`session_dir`, `session_name`, `session_url`, `session_bare_path`, `gh_session_slug`). Cleanest incremental change. None across all those = single-repo.
- `plan_init` validates new ambiguity rules:
  - `--scope=other` → fatal ("meaningless at init time").
  - Otherwise dispatch.
- Single-repo execution flow:
  - Steps 1–3: project_dir + git/jj init + write configs (only code side; uses `VC_CONFIG_APP_ONLY` / `GITIGNORE_APP_ONLY`).
  - Step 4: code template only (no `BOT` half).
  - Step 5: `Initial commit` with **no `ochid:` trailer** (no counterpart).
  - Step 6: skipped (no cross-reference needed).
  - Step 7: code-side bookmark + clean.
  - Step 8: skipped.
  - Step 9: provision + push, code side only (gates on `Provisioner`).
  - Step 10: jj re-init code side only.
  - Step 11: skipped (Claude Code symlink is session-specific).

**Single-repo fixture naming:** in `--scope=app --repo-local PARENT`, the single bare repo lands at `<PARENT>/remote.git` (not `remote-code.git`) — there's no session side to contrast with, so the `-code` suffix would be misleading. Calling this out so you can veto if you want consistency over clarity.

**Tests:**
- `--scope` parsing (all three values, default).
- `plan_init` for `--scope=app` (default mode + `--repo-local` + `--repo-remote`).
- `--scope=other` rejection.
- Single-repo fixture layout assertion (no `.claude/` dir, single bare repo at `remote.git`).
- `VC_CONFIG_APP_ONLY` / `GITIGNORE_APP_ONLY` content.

**`Cargo.toml`** — `0.40.0-1` → `0.40.0-2`.

**`notes/chores-06.md`** — append `### 0.40.0-2: init --scope` subsection (shipped surface).

**`notes/todo.md`** — In Progress entry stays; no new ref needed.

## Open questions

1. **Fixture bare-repo name in single mode** — `remote.git` (my pick) or `remote-code.git` (consistent with dual mode)?
2. **`--use-template`'s `CODE,BOT` form with `--scope=app`** — I'd reject the comma form (no BOT half applies); single CODE only. OK?
3. **`--scope=other` error message wording** — propose: `"--scope=other is meaningless at init time — use --scope=app for single-repo or --scope=both (default) for dual-repo"`.

If this looks right (and your call on the open questions), say go.

> DEVELOPER

overall looks good but what if rather than "both" we used the "--use-template" model --scope=code,bot so we'd say --scope=code or --scope=bot or --scope=code,bot, thoughts?

> AGENT

Yes — `code,bot` / `code` / `bot` / `code,bot` is better. Reasons:

- **Concrete to what the repos actually are.** Every vc-x1 workspace has a code side and a Claude bot side. `app|other|both` is deliberately abstract; `code|bot` is honest about the domain.
- **Matches `--use-template CODE[,BOT]`.** Users already think in `code,bot` terms because of that flag. One vocabulary across the CLI.
- **Extensible without a syntax change.** If a future project adds a third role (docs repo, infra repo, whatever), it's `--scope=code,bot,docs` — no new keyword required. `both` maxes out at two.
- **`--scope=bot` at init errors naturally** — "can't init just the bot side without code" reads better than "can't init just 'other'".

The `app|other|both` language was captured in earlier design sections (chores-06 "design" entries, and the refinements in 0.37.8) before implementation. Changing it at implementation time is fine — those captures were working notes, not contracts. I'll note the vocabulary change in the 0.40.0-2 chore subsection rather than rewriting the old design sections, so the record of how thinking evolved stays visible.

## Implementation shape

**`src/scope.rs`:**
```rust
pub enum Side { Code, Bot }        // clap ValueEnum
pub struct Scope(pub Vec<Side>);   // newtype over the parsed list
```

Clap side: `#[arg(long, value_delimiter = ',')] pub scope: Option<Vec<Side>>`. Parsing yields `[Code]`, `[Bot]`, or `[Code, Bot]` (order and dup handled by a normalizer). `None` → workspace-state default (for init: `[Code, Bot]`).

Three helpers in `Scope`:
- `is_code()` → single code side.
- `is_bot()` → single bot side.
- `is_both()` → both sides.

All my `-2` plan points translate directly — just s/app/code/, s/other/bot/, s/both/code,bot/. Single-repo fixture bare-repo name becomes `<PARENT>/remote.git` (unchanged — there's still only one bare in single-code mode). The `--scope=bot` rejection message becomes `"--scope=bot is meaningless at init time — use --scope=code for single-repo or --scope=code,bot (default) for dual-repo"`.

## Follow-on note (out of scope for `-2`)

Internal code uses `session_*` naming (e.g. `session_dir`, `VC_CONFIG_SESSION`) for the bot side. Adopting `--scope=bot` creates a small naming split: CLI says "bot", internals say "session". Worth a cleanup later (`s/session_/bot_/` in init.rs, plus the `.vc-config.toml` field `other-repo` maybe becoming `bot-repo`). I'd defer this to its own cycle — step `-2` stays focused on the flag surface.

## Answers to the earlier open questions, under the new vocabulary

1. **Fixture bare-repo name in `--scope=code --repo-local`**: `<PARENT>/remote.git` (no "-code" suffix — there's no bot side to contrast with). Still my pick.
2. **`--use-template CODE,BOT` with `--scope=code`**: reject the comma form; require single CODE only. Ambiguity rule.
3. **Error message for `--scope=bot` at init**: above.

If you're good with all this, say go.

> DEVELOPER

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/src/scope.rs",
  "content": "//! Which side(s) of a workspace a command should operate on.\n//!\n//! - `Side` is the clap `ValueEnum` — one of `code`, `bot`.\n//! - `Scope` wraps the parsed list; a missing flag (None at the arg\n//!   layer) means \"caller decides\", typically via workspace-state\n//!   default resolution.\n//! - See `notes/chores-06.md > Generalize --scope across commands\n//!   (0.40.0)` for the full rationale and the vocabulary history.\n\nuse clap::ValueEnum;\n\n/// One side of the workspace.\n///\n/// - `Code` — the primary (app) repo.\n/// - `Bot` — the Claude Code session repo (typically at `.claude/`).\n#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, ValueEnum)]\npub enum Side {\n    Code,\n    Bot,\n}\n\n/// Parsed `--scope` value: the set of sides the command applies to.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct Scope(pub Vec<Side>);\n\nimpl Scope {\n    /// True when the scope includes the code side.\n    pub fn has_code(&self) -> bool {\n        self.0.contains(&Side::Code)\n    }\n\n    /// True when the scope includes the bot side.\n    pub fn has_bot(&self) -> bool {\n        self.0.contains(&Side::Bot)\n    }\n\n    /// Exactly the code side — single-repo operation.\n    pub fn is_code_only(&self) -> bool {\n        self.has_code() && !self.has_bot()\n    }\n\n    /// Exactly the bot side — single-repo operation on the bot.\n    pub fn is_bot_only(&self) -> bool {\n        !self.has_code() && self.has_bot()\n    }\n\n    /// Both sides — dual-repo operation.\n    pub fn is_both(&self) -> bool {\n        self.has_code() && self.has_bot()\n    }\n\n    /// Neither side — empty scope, always an invalid input.\n    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {\n        self.0.is_empty()\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn code_only() {\n        let s = Scope(vec![Side::Code]);\n        assert!(s.is_code_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_bot_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_both());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn bot_only() {\n        let s = Scope(vec![Side::Bot]);\n        assert!(!s.is_code_only());\n        assert!(s.is_bot_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_both());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn both_code_then_bot() {\n        let s = Scope(vec![Side::Code, Side::Bot]);\n        assert!(!s.is_code_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_bot_only());\n        assert!(s.is_both());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn both_bot_then_code() {\n        // Order doesn't matter — contains-based checks.\n        let s = Scope(vec![Side::Bot, Side::Code]);\n        assert!(s.is_both());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn empty() {\n        let s = Scope(vec![]);\n        assert!(s.is_empty());\n        assert!(!s.is_code_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_bot_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_both());\n    }\n}\n"
}

577	pub(crate) fn plan_init(args: &InitArgs) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
578	    // --- Mutual-exclusion gates ---
579	    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.repo_remote.is_some() {
580	        return Err("--repo-local and --repo-remote are mutually exclusive".into());
581	    }
582	    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.dir.is_some() {
583	        return Err(
584	            "--repo-local and --dir are mutually exclusive (--repo-local is the parent)".into(),
585	        );
586	    }
587	    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.owner.is_some() {
588	        return Err(
589	            "--repo-local and --owner are mutually exclusive (fixture mode has no GitHub)".into(),
590	        );
591	    }
592	    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.private {
593	        return Err(
594	            "--repo-local and --private are mutually exclusive (fixture mode has no GitHub)".into(),
595	        );
596	    }
597	    if args.repo_remote.is_some() && args.owner.is_some() {
598	        return Err(
599	            "--repo-remote and --owner are mutually exclusive (URL already encodes owner)".into(),
600	        );
601	    }
602	    if args.repo_remote.is_some() && args.private {
603	        return Err(
604	            "--repo-remote and --private are mutually exclusive (visibility is set at repo creation, not here)"
605	                .into(),
606	        );
607	    }
608	
609	    match (&args.repo_local, &args.repo_remote) {
610	        (Some(parent_spec), None) => plan_local_fixture(args, parent_spec),
611	        (None, Some(url_spec)) => plan_external_remote(args, url_spec),
612	        (None, None) => plan_default_github(args),
613	        #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
614	        (Some(_), Some(_)) => {
615	            // OK: mutual-exclusion gate above already errored on this case
616	            unreachable!("mutual-exclusion gate earlier in plan_init")
617	        }
618	    }
619	}
620	
621	/// Plan for `--repo-local PARENT` — fixture mode.
622	///
623	/// - Bare repos and project all live under PARENT.
624	/// - No network involvement.
625	fn plan_local_fixture(
626	    args: &InitArgs,
627	    parent_spec: &str,
628	) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
629	    let name = args
630	        .name
631	        .clone()
632	        .ok_or("NAME is required with --repo-local")?;
633	    let parent = normalize_local_parent(parent_spec)?;
634	    let project_dir = parent.join(&name);
635	    let session_dir = project_dir.join(".claude");
636	    let code_bare = parent.join("remote-code.git");
637	    let session_bare = parent.join("remote-claude.git");
638	    let session_name = format!("{name}.claude");
639	    Ok(InitPlan {
640	        code_url: code_bare.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
641	        session_url: session_bare.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
642	        code_bare_path: Some(code_bare),
643	        session_bare_path: Some(session_bare),
644	        project_dir,
645	        session_dir,
646	        name,
647	        session_name,
648	        provisioner: Provisioner::LocalBareInit,
649	        gh_code_slug: None,
650	        gh_session_slug: None,
651	    })
652	}
653	
654	/// Plan for `--repo-remote URL` — explicit URL override.
655	///
656	/// - Session URL derived from the code URL.
657	/// - NAME derived from URL when not given (must agree if both).
658	/// - GitHub URLs → `GhCreate`; other URLs → `ExternalPreExisting`.
659	fn plan_external_remote(
660	    args: &InitArgs,
661	    url_spec: &str,
662	) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
663	    let normalized = normalize_remote(url_spec)?;
664	    let code_url = ensure_git_suffix(&normalized);
665	    let session_url = derive_session_url(&code_url);
666	    let url_name = derive_name_from_url(&code_url)?;
667	
668	    let name = match &args.name {
669	        Some(n) if *n == url_name => n.clone(),
670	        Some(n) => {
671	            return Err(format!(
672	                "NAME '{n}' disagrees with --repo-remote URL '{url_spec}' (derived '{url_name}')"
673	            )
674	            .into());
675	        }
676	        None => url_name.clone(),
677	    };
678	    let session_name = format!("{name}.claude");
679	
680	    let parent_dir = match &args.dir {
681	        Some(d) => d.clone(),
682	        None => std::env::current_dir()?,
683	    };
684	    let project_dir = parent_dir.join(&name);
685	    let session_dir = project_dir.join(".claude");
686	
687	    let provisioner = if is_github_url(&code_url) {
688	        Provisioner::GhCreate
689	    } else {
690	        Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting
691	    };
692	
693	    let (gh_code_slug, gh_session_slug) = if provisioner == Provisioner::GhCreate {
694	        (
695	            Some(github_slug_from_url(&code_url)?),
696	            Some(github_slug_from_url(&session_url)?),
697	        )
698	    } else {
699	        (None, None)
700	    };
701	
702	    Ok(InitPlan {
703	        project_dir,
704	        session_dir,
705	        name,
706	        session_name,
707	        code_url,
708	        session_url,
709	        provisioner,
710	        code_bare_path: None,
711	        session_bare_path: None,
712	        gh_code_slug,
713	        gh_session_slug,
714	    })
715	}
716	
717	/// Plan for default mode (no `--repo-local` / `--repo-remote`).
718	///
719	/// - NAME required.
720	/// - `owner` derived via `gh whoami` unless `--owner` given.
721	/// - Provisioner is always `GhCreate`.

742	    };
743	    let project_dir = parent_dir.join(&name);

745

755	        session_bare_path: None,
756	        gh_code_slug: Some(gh_code_slug),
757	        gh_session_slug: Some(gh_session_slug),
758	    })
759	}

(dual only).\n/// - NAME derived from URL when not given (must agree if both).\n/// - GitHub URLs → `GhCreate`; other URLs → `ExternalPreExisting`.\nfn plan_external_remote(\n    args: &InitArgs,\n    url_spec: &str,\n    scope: Scope,\n) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let normalized = normalize_remote(url_spec)?;\n    let code_url = ensure_git_suffix(&normalized);\n    let url_name = derive_name_from_url(&code_url)?;\n\n    let name = match &args.name {\n        Some(n) if *n == url_name => n.clone(),\n        Some(n) => {\n            return Err(format!(\n                \"NAME '{n}' disagrees with --repo-remote URL '{url_spec}' (derived '{url_name}')\"\n            )\n            .into());\n        }\n        None => url_name.clone(),\n    };\n\n    let parent_dir = match &args.dir {\n        Some(d) => d.clone(),\n        None => std::env::current_dir()?,\n    };\n    let project_dir = parent_dir.join(&name);\n\n    let provisioner = if is_github_url(&code_url) {\n        Provisioner::GhCreate\n    } else {\n        Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting\n    };\n\n    let gh_code_slug = if provisioner == Provisioner::GhCreate {\n        Some(github_slug_from_url(&code_url)?)\n    } else {\n        None\n    };\n\n    if scope.is_code_only() {\n        return Ok(InitPlan {\n            scope,\n            project_dir,\n            name,\n            code_url,\n            provisioner,\n            code_bare_path: None,\n            gh_code_slug,\n            session_dir: None,\n            session_name: None,\n            session_url: None,\n            session_bare_path: None,\n            gh_session_slug: None,\n        });\n    }\n\n    // Dual.\n    let session_url = derive_session_url(&code_url);\n    let session_name = format!(\"{name}.claude\");\n    let session_dir = project_dir.join(\".claude\");\n    let gh_session_slug = if provisioner == Provisioner::GhCreate {\n        Some(github_slug_from_url(&session_url)?)\n    } else {\n        None\n    };\n\n    Ok(InitPlan {\n        scope,\n        project_dir,\n        name,\n        code_url,\n        provisioner,\n        code_bare_path: None,\n        gh_code_slug,\n        session_url: Some(session_url),\n        session_name: Some(session_name),\n        session_dir: Some(session_dir),\n        session_bare_path: None,\n        gh_session_slug,\n    })\n}\n\n/// Plan for default mode (no `--repo-local` / `--repo-remote`).\n///\n/// - NAME required.\n/// - `owner` derived via `gh whoami` unless `--owner` given.\n/// - Provisioner is always `GhCreate`.\nfn plan_default_github(\n    args: &InitArgs,\n    scope: Scope,\n) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let name = args\n        .name\n        .clone()\n        .ok_or(\"NAME is required (or pass --repo-remote URL)\")?;\n\n    let owner = match &args.owner {\n        Some(o) => o.clone(),\n        None => gh_whoami()?,\n    };\n\n    let code_url = format!(\"git@github.com:{owner}/{name}.git\");\n    let gh_code_slug = format!(\"{owner}/{name}\");\n\n    let parent_dir = match &args.dir {\n        Some(d) => d.clone(),\n        None => std::env::current_dir()?,\n    };\n    let project_dir = parent_dir.join(&name);\n\n    if scope.is_code_only() {\n        return Ok(InitPlan {\n            scope,\n            project_dir,\n            name,\n            code_url,\n            provisioner: Provisioner::GhCreate,\n            code_bare_path: None,\n            gh_code_slug: Some(gh_code_slug),\n            session_dir: None,\n            session_name: None,\n            session_url: None,\n            session_bare_path: None,\n            gh_session_slug: None,\n        });\n    }\n\n    let session_name = format!(\"{name}.claude\");\n    let session_url = format!(\"git@github.com:{owner}/{session_name}.git\");\n    let gh_session_slug = format!(\"{owner}/{session_name}\");\n    let session_dir = project_dir.join(\".claude\");\n\n    Ok(InitPlan {\n        scope,\n        project_dir,\n        name,\n        code_url,\n        provisioner: Provisioner::GhCreate,\n        code_bare_path: None,\n        gh_code_slug: Some(gh_code_slug),\n        session_url: Some(session_url),\n        session_name: Some(session_name),\n        session_dir: Some(session_dir),\n        session_bare_path: None,\n        gh_session_slug: Some(gh_session_slug),\n    })\n}"
}

97	/// Run a command with retries, sleeping between attempts.
98	fn run_retry(
99	    cmd: &str,
100	    args: &[&str],
101	    cwd: &Path,
102	    retries: u32,
103	    delay_secs: u64,
104	) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
105	    let mut last_err = String::new();
106	    for attempt in 1..=retries {
107	        match run(cmd, args, cwd) {
108	            Ok(out) => {
109	                if attempt > 1 {
110	                    debug!("succeeded after {attempt} attempts");
111	                }
112	                return Ok(out);
113	            }
114	            Err(e) => {
115	                last_err = e.to_string();
116	                if attempt < retries {
117	                    debug!("attempt {attempt}/{retries} failed: {last_err}");
118	                    debug!("retrying in {delay_secs}s...");
119	                    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(delay_secs));
120	                }
121	            }
122	        }
123	    }
124	    Err(format!("failed after {retries} attempts: {last_err}").into())
125	}

127	use crate::common::{mkdir_p, run, write_file};
