129	/// Parse the `--use-template` value into `(code, bot)` template paths.
130	///
131	/// Format: `CODE[,BOT]`. If `BOT` is omitted, the default is the sibling
132	/// directory whose name is `<CODE-basename>.claude` (via
133	/// `Path::with_file_name`, so a trailing slash on `CODE` does not produce
134	/// a different result).
135	pub(crate) fn parse_use_template(
136	    s: &str,
137	) -> Result<(PathBuf, PathBuf), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
138	    let mut parts = s.splitn(2, ',');
139	    let code_raw = parts.next().unwrap_or(""); // OK: splitn always yields at least one element
140	    let bot_raw = parts.next();
141	    let code_trim = code_raw.trim();
142	    if code_trim.is_empty() {
143	        return Err("--use-template: code template path is empty".into());
144	    }
145	    let code = PathBuf::from(code_trim);
146	    let bot = match bot_raw.map(str::trim) {
147	        Some(b) if !b.is_empty() => PathBuf::from(b),
148	        _ => {
149	            let file_name = code.file_name().ok_or_else(|| {
150	                format!(
151	                    "--use-template: cannot derive default bot path from '{}' (no file name component)",
152	                    code.display()
153	                )
154	            })?;
155	            let new_name = format!("{}.claude", file_name.to_string_lossy());
156	            code.with_file_name(new_name)
157	        }
158	    };
159	    Ok((code, bot))
160	}
161	
162	/// Top-level non-hidden files init writes. Kept here so that if init is
163	/// ever extended to write non-hidden top-level files, the pre-flight
164	/// conflict scan flags any template that would clash. Currently empty
165	/// because init only writes hidden files (`.vc-config.toml`, `.gitignore`),
166	/// and the template copy skips hidden entries.
167	const RESERVED_TEMPLATE_ENTRIES: &[&str] = &[];
168	
169	/// Validate that both template paths exist, are directories, and contain no
170	/// top-level non-hidden entry that would collide with a file init writes.
171	pub(crate) fn validate_templates(
172	    code: &Path,
173	    bot: &Path,
174	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
175	    for (label, p) in [("code", code), ("bot", bot)] {
176	        if !p.exists() {
177	            return Err(format!(
178	                "--use-template: {label} template '{}' does not exist",
179	                p.display()
180	            )
181	            .into());
182	        }
183	        if !p.is_dir() {
184	            return Err(format!(
185	                "--use-template: {label} template '{}' is not a directory",
186	                p.display()
187	            )
188	            .into());
189	        }
190	        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(p)? {
191	            let entry = entry?;
192	            let name = entry.file_name();
193	            let name_str = name.to_string_lossy();
194	            if name_str.starts_with('.') {
195	                continue;
196	            }
197	            if RESERVED_TEMPLATE_ENTRIES.contains(&name_str.as_ref()) {
198	                return Err(format!(
199	                    "--use-template: {label} template '{}' contains reserved entry '{}'",
200	                    p.display(),
201	                    name_str
202	                )
203	                .into());
204	            }
205	        }
206	    }
207	    Ok(())
208	}
209	
210	/// Recursively copy non-hidden entries from `src` to `dst`. Any entry whose
211	/// file name starts with `.` is skipped. Symlinks are skipped with a debug
212	/// log — templates don't need them, and following them risks escaping the
213	/// template tree.
214	pub(crate) fn copy_template_recursive(
215	    src: &Path,
216	    dst: &Path,
217	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
218	    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(src)? {
219	        let entry = entry?;
220	        let name = entry.file_name();
221	        let name_str = name.to_string_lossy();
222	        if name_str.starts_with('.') {
223	            continue;
224	        }
225	        let src_path = entry.path();
226	        let dst_path = dst.join(&name);
227	        let ft = entry.file_type()?;
228	        if ft.is_symlink() {
229	            debug!("skip symlink {}", src_path.display());
230	            continue;
231	        }
232	        if ft.is_dir() {
233	            mkdir_p(&dst_path)?;
234	            copy_template_recursive(&src_path, &dst_path)?;
235	        } else if ft.is_file() {
236	            debug!("copy {} -> {}", src_path.display(), dst_path.display());
237	            std::fs::copy(&src_path, &dst_path)?;
238	        }
239	    }
240	    Ok(())
241	}
242	
243	/// Replace the first line of `<dir>/README.md` with `# <name>`. If
244	/// `README.md` is absent, this is a no-op. Trailing content after the
245	/// first newline is preserved verbatim; a file with no newline becomes
246	/// just `# <name>`.
247	pub(crate) fn rewrite_readme_first_line(
248	    dir: &Path,
249	    name: &str,
250	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
251	    let readme = dir.join("README.md");
252	    if !readme.exists() {
253	        return Ok(());
254	    }
255	    let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&readme)?;
256	    let rest = match content.find('\n') {
257	        Some(pos) => &content[pos..],
258	        None => "",
259	    };
260	    let new_content = format!("# {name}{rest}");
261	    std::fs::write(&readme, new_content)?;
262	    debug!("rewrote first line of {}", readme.display());
263	    Ok(())
264	}

266	/// Get the short (12-char) jj change ID for a revision, without printing.
267	fn jj_chid(rev: &str, cwd: &Path) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
268	    let full = run(
269	        "jj",
270	        &[
271	            "log",
272	            "-r",
273	            rev,
274	            "--no-graph",
275	            "-T",
276	            "change_id",
277	            "--limit",
278	            "1",
279	        ],
280	        cwd,
281	    )?;
282	    Ok(full[..full.len().min(12)].to_string())
283	}

352	/// Expand `~` and `$VAR` / `${VAR}` substitutions in a user string.
353	///
354	/// - `~` and `~/…` resolve via `HOME`.
355	/// - Unset env vars are a fatal error (silent empty substitution
356	///   would mask typos).
357	/// - Bare `$` with no identifier after stays literal.
358	pub(crate) fn expand_vars(s: &str) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
359	    let home_expanded = if s == "~" {
360	        std::env::var("HOME").map_err(|_| "HOME is not set; cannot expand '~'")?
361	    } else if let Some(rest) = s.strip_prefix("~/") {
362	        let home = std::env::var("HOME").map_err(|_| "HOME is not set; cannot expand '~/'")?;
363	        format!("{home}/{rest}")
364	    } else {
365	        s.to_string()
366	    };
367	
368	    let mut out = String::with_capacity(home_expanded.len());
369	    let mut chars = home_expanded.chars().peekable();
370	    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
371	        if c != '$' {
372	            out.push(c);
373	            continue;
374	        }
375	        let (name, braced) = if chars.peek() == Some(&'{') {
376	            chars.next(); // OK: peeked '{' above, next() must yield it
377	            let mut name = String::new();
378	            let mut closed = false;
379	            for c2 in chars.by_ref() {
380	                if c2 == '}' {
381	                    closed = true;
382	                    break;
383	                }
384	                name.push(c2);
385	            }
386	            if !closed {
387	                return Err(format!("unterminated '${{…}}' in '{s}'").into());
388	            }
389	            (name, true)
390	        } else {
391	            let mut name = String::new();
392	            while let Some(&c2) = chars.peek() {
393	                if c2.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c2 == '_' {
394	                    name.push(c2);
395	                    chars.next();
396	                } else {
397	                    break;
398	                }
399	            }
400	            (name, false)
```

416	/// True when `s` looks like a git remote URL rather than a path.
417	///
418	/// - Scheme-qualified (`scheme://…`).
419	/// - scp-like SSH form (`user@host:path`).
420	/// - Everything else is treated as a path.
421	pub(crate) fn is_remote_url(s: &str) -> bool {
422	    if s.contains("://") {
423	        return true;
424	    }
425	    if let Some(at) = s.find('@')
426	        && let Some(colon) = s.find(':')
427	        && at < colon
428	        && !s[..at].contains('/')
429	    {
430	        return true;
431	    }
432	    false
433	}
434	
435	/// True when `s` is a GitHub URL (host-based detection).
436	///
437	/// - SSH scp-like (`git@github.com:…`).
438	/// - `ssh://git@github.com/…`.
439	/// - `https://github.com/…` and `http://github.com/…`.
440	/// - GitHub URLs route to the `gh repo create` provisioner.
441	pub(crate) fn is_github_url(s: &str) -> bool {
442	    s.starts_with("git@github.com:")
443	        || s.starts_with("ssh://git@github.com/")
444	        || s.starts_with("https://github.com/")
445	        || s.starts_with("http://github.com/")
446	}
447	
448	/// Normalize a `--repo-remote` value. Expand variables, then for
449	/// paths make absolute; URLs pass through after expansion.
450	pub(crate) fn normalize_remote(s: &str) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
451	    let s = s.trim();
452	    if s.is_empty() {
453	        return Err("--repo-remote: value is empty".into());
454	    }
455	    let expanded = expand_vars(s)?;
456	    if is_remote_url(&expanded) {
457	        return Ok(expanded);
458	    }
459	    let p = PathBuf::from(&expanded);
460	    if p.is_absolute() {
461	        Ok(expanded)
462	    } else {
463	        let cwd = std::env::current_dir()?;
464	        Ok(cwd.join(p).to_string_lossy().to_string())
465	    }
466	}
467	
468	/// Normalize a `--repo-local` value: expand variables, make
469	/// absolute. Parent doesn't need to exist yet — init creates it.
470	pub(crate) fn normalize_local_parent(s: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
471	    let s = s.trim();
472	    if s.is_empty() {
473	        return Err("--repo-local: value is empty".into());
474	    }
475	    let expanded = expand_vars(s)?;
476	    let p = PathBuf::from(&expanded);
477	    if p.is_absolute() {
478	        Ok(p)
479	    } else {
480	        let cwd = std::env::current_dir()?;
481	        Ok(cwd.join(p))
482	    }
483	}
484	
485	/// Append `.git` to a URL-or-path stem if not already present.
486	///
487	/// - Lets `--repo-remote git@github.com:u/tf1` normalize to the
488	///   same URL the default-mode derivation produces.
489	pub(crate) fn ensure_git_suffix(s: &str) -> String {
490	    if s.ends_with(".git") {
491	        s.to_string()
492	    } else {
493	        format!("{s}.git")
494	    }
495	}
496	
497	/// Derive the session URL from the code URL.
498	///
499	/// - With trailing `.git`: insert `.claude` before it
500	///   (`foo.git` → `foo.claude.git`).
501	/// - Without `.git`: append `.claude`.
502	pub(crate) fn derive_session_url(code_url: &str) -> String {
503	    match code_url.strip_suffix(".git") {
504	        Some(stem) => format!("{stem}.claude.git"),
505	        None => format!("{code_url}.claude"),
506	    }
507	}
508	
509	/// Derive the project NAME from a URL.
510	///
511	/// - Last path segment, trailing `.git` stripped.
512	/// - Segment boundary is the rightmost `/` or `:` (scp-like SSH).
513	pub(crate) fn derive_name_from_url(url: &str) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
514	    let stem = url.strip_suffix(".git").unwrap_or(url);
515	    let last = stem.rsplit(['/', ':']).next().unwrap_or(""); // OK: rsplit always yields at least one element
516	    if last.is_empty() {
517	        return Err(format!("cannot derive project name from URL '{url}'").into());
518	    }
519	    Ok(last.to_string())
520	}
521	
522	/// How init should provision the remote repositories it pushes to.
523	/// See `notes/chores-06.md > Generalize --scope across commands
524	/// (0.40.0) > 0.40.0-1` for the dispatch rules.
525	#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
526	pub(crate) enum Provisioner {
527	    /// `git init --bare` on local paths under `--repo-local`.
528	    LocalBareInit,
529	    /// `gh repo create` for GitHub URLs (default mode or
530	    /// `--repo-remote <github-url>`).
531	    GhCreate,
532	    /// Skip any create step; the caller pre-created the remote
533	    /// (non-GitHub URLs under `--repo-remote`).
534	    ExternalPreExisting,
535	}
536	
537	/// Fully-resolved inputs to the init execution phase.
538	///
539	/// - Dispatch and ambiguity rules live in `plan_init`.
540	/// - Downstream code operates on an `InitPlan` — execution stays
541	///   linear.
542	#[derive(Debug)]
543	pub(crate) struct InitPlan {
544	    /// Which side(s) this plan creates. `scope.is_code_only()` means
545	    /// single-repo; `scope.is_both()` means dual.
546	    pub scope: Scope,
547	    pub project_dir: PathBuf,
548	    pub name: String,
549	    pub code_url: String,
550	    pub provisioner: Provisioner,
551	    /// Set only when `provisioner == LocalBareInit`.
552	    pub code_bare_path: Option<PathBuf>,
553	    /// GitHub `owner/name` for the code side; only populated for
554	    /// the `GhCreate` path (`gh repo create` needs it).
555	    pub gh_code_slug: Option<String>,
556	    /// Session-side dir. None when `scope.is_code_only()`.
557	    pub session_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
558	    /// Session-side project name (e.g. `<name>.claude`). None when
559	    /// `scope.is_code_only()`.
560	    pub session_name: Option<String>,
561	    /// Session-side origin URL. None when `scope.is_code_only()`.
562	    pub session_url: Option<String>,
563	    /// Session-side bare-repo path. `Some` only under `LocalBareInit`
564	    /// with `scope.is_both()`.
565	    pub session_bare_path: Option<PathBuf>,
566	    /// Session-side GitHub slug. `Some` only under `GhCreate` with
567	    /// `scope.is_both()`.
568	    pub gh_session_slug: Option<String>,
569	}
570	
571	/// Build an `InitPlan` from CLI args.
572	///
573	/// - Validate flag mutual-exclusion.
574	/// - Resolve URLs and paths; derive NAME from URL if not given.
575	/// - Pick the provisioner.
576	/// - Errors on any ambiguous or incomplete flag combination.
577	pub(crate) fn plan_init(args: &InitArgs) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
578	    // --- Resolve scope first: default (None) → dual; empty Vec is
579	    // invalid; `bot` alone is meaningless at init time. ---
580	    let scope = match &args.scope {
581	        None => Scope(vec![Side::Code, Side::Bot]),
582	        Some(sides) => {
583	            let s = Scope(sides.clone());
584	            if s.is_empty() {
585	                return Err("--scope: value is empty".into());
586	            }
587	            if s.is_bot_only() {
588	                return Err(
589	                    "--scope=bot is meaningless at init time — use --scope=code for single-repo or --scope=code,bot (default) for dual-repo"
590	                        .into(),
591	                );
592	            }
593	            s
594	        }
595	    };
596	
597	    // --- Mutual-exclusion gates (flag-pair ambiguity) ---
598	    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.repo_remote.is_some() {
599	        return Err("--repo-local and --repo-remote are mutually exclusive".into());
600	    }
601	    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.dir.is_some() {
602	        return Err(
603	            "--repo-local and --dir are mutually exclusive (--repo-local is the parent)".into(),
604	        );
605	    }
606	    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.owner.is_some() {
607	        return Err(
608	            "--repo-local and --owner are mutually exclusive (fixture mode has no GitHub)".into(),
609	        );
610	    }
611	    if args.repo_local.is_some() && args.private {
612	        return Err(
613	            "--repo-local and --private are mutually exclusive (fixture mode has no GitHub)".into(),
614	        );
615	    }
616	    if args.repo_remote.is_some() && args.owner.is_some() {
617	        return Err(
618	            "--repo-remote and --owner are mutually exclusive (URL already encodes owner)".into(),
619	        );
620	    }
621	    if args.repo_remote.is_some() && args.private {
622	        return Err(
623	            "--repo-remote and --private are mutually exclusive (visibility is set at repo creation, not here)"
624	                .into(),
625	        );
626	    }
627	
628	    // --- Scope-dependent ambiguity. ---
629	    if scope.is_code_only()
630	        && let Some(t) = &args.use_template
631	        && t.contains(',')
632	    {
633	        return Err(format!(
634	            "--scope=code takes a single template path; got '{t}' (drop the `,BOT` half)"
635	        )
636	        .into());
637	    }
638	
639	    match (&args.repo_local, &args.repo_remote) {
640	        (Some(parent_spec), None) => plan_local_fixture(args, parent_spec, scope),
641	        (None, Some(url_spec)) => plan_external_remote(args, url_spec, scope),
642	        (None, None) => plan_default_github(args, scope),
643	        #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
644	        (Some(_), Some(_)) => {
645	            // OK: mutual-exclusion gate above already errored on this case
646	            unreachable!("mutual-exclusion gate earlier in plan_init")
647	        }
648	    }
649	}
650	
651	/// Plan for `--repo-local PARENT` — fixture mode.
652	///
653	/// - Code-only: single bare at `<PARENT>/remote.git`, no session.
654	/// - Dual: `remote-code.git` + `remote-claude.git` plus a nested
655	///   `.claude/` session.
656	fn plan_local_fixture(
657	    args: &InitArgs,
658	    parent_spec: &str,
659	    scope: Scope,
660	) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
661	    let name = args
662	        .name
663	        .clone()
664	        .ok_or("NAME is required with --repo-local")?;
665	    let parent = normalize_local_parent(parent_spec)?;
666	    let project_dir = parent.join(&name);
667	
668	    if scope.is_code_only() {
669	        let code_bare = parent.join("remote.git");
670	        return Ok(InitPlan {
671	            scope,
672	            code_url: code_bare.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
673	            code_bare_path: Some(code_bare),
674	            project_dir,
675	            name,
676	            provisioner: Provisioner::LocalBareInit,
677	            gh_code_slug: None,
678	            session_dir: None,
679	            session_name: None,
680	            session_url: None,
681	            session_bare_path: None,
682	            gh_session_slug: None,
683	        });
684	    }
685	
686	    // Dual: code + bot sides, nested session.
687	    let session_dir = project_dir.join(".claude");
688	    let code_bare = parent.join("remote-code.git");
689	    let session_bare = parent.join("remote-claude.git");
690	    let session_name = format!("{name}.claude");
691	    Ok(InitPlan {
692	        scope,
693	        code_url: code_bare.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
694	        code_bare_path: Some(code_bare),
695	        project_dir,
696	        name,
697	        provisioner: Provisioner::LocalBareInit,
698	        gh_code_slug: None,
699	        session_url: Some(session_bare.to_string_lossy().to_string()),
700	        session_bare_path: Some(session_bare),
701	        session_dir: Some(session_dir),
702	        session_name: Some(session_name),
703	        gh_session_slug: None,
704	    })
705	}
706	
707	/// Plan for `--repo-remote URL` — explicit URL override.
708	///
709	/// - Session URL derived (dual only).
710	/// - NAME derived from URL when not given (must agree if both).
711	/// - GitHub URLs → `GhCreate`; other URLs → `ExternalPreExisting`.
712	fn plan_external_remote(
713	    args: &InitArgs,
714	    url_spec: &str,
715	    scope: Scope,
716	) -> Result<InitPlan, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
717	    let normalized = normalize_remote(url_spec)?;
718	    let code_url = ensure_git_suffix(&normalized);
719	    let url_name = derive_name_from_url(&code_url)?;
720	
721	    let name = match &args.name {
722	        Some(n) if *n == url_name => n.clone(),
723	        Some(n) => {
724	            return Err(format!(
725	                "NAME '{n}' disagrees with --repo-remote URL '{url_spec}' (derived '{url_name}')"
726	            )
727	            .into());
728	        }
729	        None => url_name.clone(),
730	    };
731	
732	    let parent_dir = match &args.dir {
733	        Some(d) => d.clone(),
734	        None => std::env::current_dir()?,
735	    };
736	    let project_dir = parent_dir.join(&name);
737	
738	    let provisioner = if is_github_url(&code_url) {
739	        Provisioner::GhCreate
740	    } else {
741	        Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting
742	    };
743	
744	    let gh_code_slug = if provisioner == Provisioner::GhCreate {
745	        Some(github_slug_from_url(&code_url)?)
746	    } else {
747	        None
748	    };
749	
750	    if scope.is_code_only() {
751	        return Ok(InitPlan {
752	            scope,
753	            project_dir,
754	            name,
755	            code_url,
756	            provisioner,
757	            code_bare_path: None,
758	            gh_code_slug,
759	            session_dir: None,
760	            session_name: None,
761	            session_url: None,
762	            session_bare_path: None,
763	            gh_session_slug: None,
764	        });
765	    }
766	
767	    // Dual.
768	    let session_url = derive_session_url(&code_url);
769	    let session_name = format!("{name}.claude");
770	    let session_dir = project_dir.join(".claude");
771	    let gh_session_slug = if provisioner == Provisioner::GhCreate {
772	        Some(github_slug_from_url(&session_url)?)
773	    } else {
774	        None
775	    };
776	
777	    Ok(InitPlan {
778	        scope,
779	        project_dir,
780	        name,
781	        code_url,
782	        provisioner,
783	        code_bare_path: None,
784	        gh_code_slug,
785	        session_url: Some(session_url),
786	        session_name: Some(session_name),
787	        session_dir: Some(session_dir),
788	        session_bare_path: None,
789	        gh_session_slug,
790	    })
791	}
792	
793	/// Plan for default mode (no `--repo-local` / `--repo-remote`).
794	///
795	/// - NAME required.
796	/// - `owner` derived via `gh whoami` unless `--owner` given.
797	/// - Provisioner is always `GhCreate`.
798	fn plan_default_github(
799	    args: &InitArgs,

859	/// Extract the `owner/name` slug from a GitHub URL (any of the
860	/// supported forms: SSH scp-like, `ssh://`, `https://`).
861	fn github_slug_from_url(url: &str) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
862	    let stem = url.strip_suffix(".git").unwrap_or(url);
863	    // The slug is the last two path components, `owner/name`.
864	    if let Some(path) = stem
865	        .strip_prefix("git@github.com:")
866	        .or_else(|| stem.strip_prefix("ssh://git@github.com/"))
867	        .or_else(|| stem.strip_prefix("https://github.com/"))
868	        .or_else(|| stem.strip_prefix("http://github.com/"))
869	    {
870	        let parts: Vec<&str> = path.split('/').collect();
871	        if parts.len() >= 2 {
872	            return Ok(format!(
873	                "{}/{}",
874	                parts[parts.len() - 2],
875	                parts[parts.len() - 1]
876	            ));
877	        }
878	    }
879	    Err(format!("cannot extract owner/name slug from GitHub URL '{url}'").into())
880	}
881	
882	pub fn init(args: &InitArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
883	    debug!("init: enter");
884	
885	    let plan = plan_init(args)?;
886	
887	    // --- Preflight ---
888	    info!("Preflight checks...");
889	
890	    debug!("verify jj is installed");
891	    run("jj", &["--version"], Path::new(".")).map_err(|_| "jj is not installed")?;
892	
893	    if plan.provisioner == Provisioner::GhCreate {
894	        debug!("verify gh CLI is installed + authenticated");
895	        run("gh", &["auth", "status"], Path::new("."))
896	            .map_err(|_| "gh is not installed or not authenticated (run: gh auth login)")?;
897	    }
898	
899	    if plan.project_dir.exists() {
900	        return Err(format!("'{}' already exists", plan.project_dir.display()).into());
901	    }
902	
903	    match &plan.provisioner {
904	        Provisioner::GhCreate => {
905	            // Safe to unwrap: GhCreate always populates these slugs.
906	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
907	            let code_slug = plan.gh_code_slug.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: GhCreate path always sets gh_code_slug
908	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
909	            let session_slug = plan.gh_session_slug.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: GhCreate path always sets gh_session_slug
910	            let (code_owner, code_name) = split_slug(code_slug)?;
911	            let (session_owner, session_name) = split_slug(session_slug)?;
912	            if gh_repo_exists(code_owner, code_name)? {
913	                return Err(format!("GitHub repo '{code_slug}' already exists").into());
914	            }
915	            if gh_repo_exists(session_owner, session_name)? {
916	                return Err(format!("GitHub repo '{session_slug}' already exists").into());
917	            }
918	        }
919	        Provisioner::LocalBareInit => {
920	            // Safe to unwrap: LocalBareInit always populates these.
921	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
922	            let code_bare = plan.code_bare_path.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: LocalBareInit path always sets code_bare_path
923	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
924	            let session_bare = plan.session_bare_path.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: LocalBareInit path always sets session_bare_path
925	            if code_bare.exists() {
926	                return Err(format!(
927	                    "bare repo '{}' already exists; refusing to clobber",
928	                    code_bare.display()
929	                )
930	                .into());
931	            }
932	            if session_bare.exists() {
933	                return Err(format!(
934	                    "bare repo '{}' already exists; refusing to clobber",
935	                    session_bare.display()
936	                )
937	                .into());
938	            }
939	        }
940	        Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting => {
941	            // For path-shaped URLs: the path must exist (caller
942	            // pre-created with `git init --bare`). For scheme-URL
943	            // values we can't preflight cheaply — let git push
944	            // surface failures.
945	            for (label, url) in [("code", &plan.code_url), ("session", &plan.session_url)] {
946	                if !is_remote_url(url) {
947	                    let p = PathBuf::from(url);
948	                    if !p.exists() {
949	                        return Err(format!(
950	                            "--repo-remote: {label} path '{}' does not exist — \
951	                             pre-create with `git init --bare`, or use --repo-local for fixture creation",
952	                            p.display()
953	                        )
954	                        .into());
955	                    }
956	                }
957	            }
958	        }
959	    }
960	
961	    let templates = match &args.use_template {
962	        Some(s) => {
963	            let (code_t, bot_t) = parse_use_template(s)?;
964	            validate_templates(&code_t, &bot_t)?;
965	            Some((code_t, bot_t))
966	        }
967	        None => None,
968	    };
969	
970	    let visibility = if args.private {
971	        "--private"
972	    } else {
973	        "--public"
974	    };
975	
976	    if args.dry_run {
977	        info!("Dry run — would execute:");
978	        info!("  1. Create directories: {}", plan.project_dir.display());
979	        info!("  2. git init + jj git init --colocate on both repos");
980	        info!("  3. Write .vc-config.toml and .gitignore to both");
981	        match &templates {
982	            Some((c, b)) => {
983	                info!(
984	                    "  4. Copy templates (non-hidden) into both repos + rewrite README.md first line"
985	                );
986	                info!("       code: {}", c.display());
987	                info!("       bot:  {}", b.display());
988	            }
989	            None => info!("  4. (skipped — no --use-template)"),
990	        }
991	        info!("  5. jj commit both with placeholder ochids");
992	        info!("  6. Get both chids, jj describe both with correct ochids");
993	        info!("  7. Remove jj from both (git clean -xdf)");
994	        match &plan.provisioner {
995	            Provisioner::GhCreate => {
996	                info!(
997	                    "  8. gh repo create {} {visibility}; push to {}",
998	                    plan.gh_session_slug.as_deref().unwrap_or(""), // OK: GhCreate always sets this (dry-run display only)
999	                    plan.session_url
1000	                );
1001	                info!(
1002	                    "  9. gh repo create {} {visibility}; push to {}",
1003	                    plan.gh_code_slug.as_deref().unwrap_or(""), // OK: GhCreate always sets this (dry-run display only)
1004	                    plan.code_url
1005	                );
1006	            }
1007	            Provisioner::LocalBareInit => {
1008	                info!(
1009	                    "  8. git init --bare {}; push to {}",
1010	                    plan.session_bare_path

1084	    // Step 5: jj commit both with placeholder ochids

1088	        "jj",

1091	    )?;

1094	        "jj",

1096	        &plan.session_dir,

1101	    let code_chid = jj_chid("@-", &plan.project_dir)?;
1102	    let session_chid = jj_chid("@-", &plan.session_dir)?;

1108	        "jj",
1109	        &["describe", "@-", "-m", &code_desc],

1113	    run(
1114	        "jj",
1115	        &["describe", "@-", "-m", &session_desc],

1118

1131	        "jj",

1254	/// Execute Step 8 or Step 9 for one side.
1255	///
1256	/// - Provision the remote per the plan's provisioner.
1257	/// - Add `origin` and push `main` with retry.
1258	/// - Centralizing keeps both sides' step bodies identical.
1259	#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
1260	fn run_remote_step(
1261	    step_label: &str,
1262	    side_label: &str,
1263	    plan: &InitPlan,
1264	    remote_url: &str,
1265	    gh_slug: Option<&str>,
1266	    bare_path: Option<&Path>,
1267	    visibility: &str,
1268	    push_from: &Path,
1269	    args: &InitArgs,
1270	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
1271	    match &plan.provisioner {
1272	        Provisioner::GhCreate => {
1273	            // Safe: GhCreate always supplies gh_slug.
1274	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
1275	            let slug = gh_slug.unwrap(); // OK: GhCreate path always sets gh_slug
1276	            info!("{step_label}: Creating GitHub repo {slug} ({side_label})...");
1277	            debug!("create {side_label}-side remote on GitHub");
1278	            run(
1279	                "gh",
1280	                &["repo", "create", slug, visibility],
1281	                &plan.project_dir,
1282	            )?;
1283	        }
1284	        Provisioner::LocalBareInit => {
1285	            // Safe: LocalBareInit always supplies bare_path.
1286	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
1287	            let bare = bare_path.unwrap(); // OK: LocalBareInit path always sets bare_path
1288	            info!(
1289	                "{step_label}: Initializing local bare repo at {} ({side_label})...",
1290	                bare.display()
1291	            );
1292	            debug!("init {side_label}-side bare repo as the local origin");
1293	            run(
1294	                "git",
1295	                &["init", "--bare", &bare.to_string_lossy()],
1296	                Path::new("."),
1297	            )?;
1298	        }
1299	        Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting => {
1300	            info!("{step_label}: Using pre-existing {side_label} remote {remote_url}");
1301	        }
1302	    }
1303	    debug!("point {side_label}-side git at its remote");
1304	    run("git", &["remote", "add", "origin", remote_url], push_from)?;
1305	    debug!("publish {side_label}-side initial commit; retry for GhCreate's async propagation");
1306	    run_retry(
1307	        "git",
1308	        &["push", "-u", "origin", "main"],
1309	        push_from,
1310	        args.push_retries,
1311	        args.push_retry_delay,
1312	    )?;
1313	    Ok(())
1314	}
1315	
1316	/// Split an `owner/name` slug. Errors if the shape is wrong (no `/`
1317	/// or more than one `/`).
1318	fn split_slug(slug: &str) -> Result<(&str, &str), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
1319	    let parts: Vec<&str> = slug.split('/').collect();
1320	    if parts.len() != 2 || parts[0].is_empty() || parts[1].is_empty() {
1321	        return Err(format!("unexpected GitHub slug '{slug}' (want 'owner/name')").into());
1322	    }
1323	    Ok((parts[0], parts[1]))
1324	}

1474	    #[test]
1475	    fn copy_template_skips_hidden_entries() {
1476	        let root = tmp_root("copy-skip-hidden");
1477	        let src = root.join("src");
1478	        let dst = root.join("dst");
1479	        std::fs::create_dir_all(&src).unwrap();
1480	        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dst).unwrap();
1481	
1482	        // Non-hidden: visible file, visible dir with nested file.
1483	        std::fs::write(src.join("keep.txt"), "keep").unwrap();
1484	        std::fs::create_dir_all(src.join("sub")).unwrap();
1485	        std::fs::write(src.join("sub").join("nested.txt"), "nested").unwrap();
1486	
1487	        // Hidden: dotfile, dotdir (with contents that must NOT be copied).
1488	        std::fs::write(src.join(".hidden"), "should-not-copy").unwrap();
1489	        std::fs::create_dir_all(src.join(".dotdir")).unwrap();
1490	        std::fs::write(src.join(".dotdir").join("inside"), "nope").unwrap();
1491	
1492	        copy_template_recursive(&src, &dst).unwrap();
1493	
1494	        assert_eq!(
1495	            std::fs::read_to_string(dst.join("keep.txt")).unwrap(),
1496	            "keep"
1497	        );
1498	        assert_eq!(
1499	            std::fs::read_to_string(dst.join("sub").join("nested.txt")).unwrap(),
1500	            "nested"
1501	        );
1502	        assert!(!dst.join(".hidden").exists());
1503	        assert!(!dst.join(".dotdir").exists());
1504	
1505	        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
1506	    }

  "old_string": "/// Validate that both template paths exist, are directories, and contain no\n/// top-level non-hidden entry that would collide with a file init writes.\npub(crate) fn validate_templates(\n    code: &Path,\n    bot: &Path,\n) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    for (label, p) in [(\"code\", code), (\"bot\", bot)] {\n        if !p.exists() {\n            return Err(format!(\n                \"--use-template: {label} template '{}' does not exist\",\n                p.display()\n            )\n            .into());\n        }\n        if !p.is_dir() {\n            return Err(format!(\n                \"--use-template: {label} template '{}' is not a directory\",\n                p.display()\n            )\n            .into());\n        }\n        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(p)? {\n            let entry = entry?;\n            let name = entry.file_name();\n            let name_str = name.to_string_lossy();\n            if name_str.starts_with('.') {\n                continue;\n            }\n            if RESERVED_TEMPLATE_ENTRIES.contains(&name_str.as_ref()) {\n                return Err(format!(\n                    \"--use-template: {label} template '{}' contains reserved entry '{}'\",\n                    p.display(),\n                    name_str\n                )\n                .into());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Validate one template directory: exists, is a directory, and has no\n/// top-level non-hidden entry that would collide with init's own writes.\n///\n/// - `label` prefixes every error message (`code` / `bot`).\npub(crate) fn validate_template_one(\n    label: &str,\n    p: &Path,\n) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    if !p.exists() {\n        return Err(format!(\n            \"--use-template: {label} template '{}' does not exist\",\n            p.display()\n        )\n        .into());\n    }\n    if !p.is_dir() {\n        return Err(format!(\n            \"--use-template: {label} template '{}' is not a directory\",\n            p.display()\n        )\n        .into());\n    }\n    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(p)? {\n        let entry = entry?;\n        let name = entry.file_name();\n        let name_str = name.to_string_lossy();\n        if name_str.starts_with('.') {\n            continue;\n        }\n        if RESERVED_TEMPLATE_ENTRIES.contains(&name_str.as_ref()) {\n            return Err(format!(\n                \"--use-template: {label} template '{}' contains reserved entry '{}'\",\n                p.display(),\n                name_str\n            )\n            .into());\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Validate both template paths (dual-repo mode). Thin wrapper over\n/// `validate_template_one` kept for single-call ergonomics.\npub(crate) fn validate_templates(\n    code: &Path,\n    bot: &Path,\n) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    validate_template_one(\"code\", code)?;\n    validate_template_one(\"bot\", bot)?;\n    Ok(())\n}"
}

  "new_string": "pub fn init(args: &InitArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"init: enter\");\n\n    let plan = plan_init(args)?;\n    let is_dual = plan.scope.is_both();\n\n    // --- Preflight ---\n    info!(\"Preflight checks...\");\n\n    debug!(\"verify jj is installed\");\n    run(\"jj\", &[\"--version\"], Path::new(\".\")).map_err(|_| \"jj is not installed\")?;\n\n    if plan.provisioner == Provisioner::GhCreate {\n        debug!(\"verify gh CLI is installed + authenticated\");\n        run(\"gh\", &[\"auth\", \"status\"], Path::new(\".\"))\n            .map_err(|_| \"gh is not installed or not authenticated (run: gh auth login)\")?;\n    }\n\n    if plan.project_dir.exists() {\n        return Err(format!(\"'{}' already exists\", plan.project_dir.display()).into());\n    }\n\n    match &plan.provisioner {\n        Provisioner::GhCreate => {\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            let code_slug = plan.gh_code_slug.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: GhCreate path always sets gh_code_slug\n            let (code_owner, code_name) = split_slug(code_slug)?;\n            if gh_repo_exists(code_owner, code_name)? {\n                return Err(format!(\"GitHub repo '{code_slug}' already exists\").into());\n            }\n            if let Some(session_slug) = plan.gh_session_slug.as_ref() {\n                let (session_owner, session_name) = split_slug(session_slug)?;\n                if gh_repo_exists(session_owner, session_name)? {\n                    return Err(format!(\"GitHub repo '{session_slug}' already exists\").into());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Provisioner::LocalBareInit => {\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            let code_bare = plan.code_bare_path.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: LocalBareInit path always sets code_bare_path\n            if code_bare.exists() {\n                return Err(format!(\n                    \"bare repo '{}' already exists; refusing to clobber\",\n                    code_bare.display()\n                )\n                .into());\n            }\n            if let Some(session_bare) = plan.session_bare_path.as_ref()\n                && session_bare.exists()\n            {\n                return Err(format!(\n                    \"bare repo '{}' already exists; refusing to clobber\",\n                    session_bare.display()\n                )\n                .into());\n            }\n        }\n        Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting => {\n            // For path-shaped URLs: the path must exist (caller\n            // pre-created with `git init --bare`). For scheme-URL\n            // values we can't preflight cheaply — let git push\n            // surface failures.\n            if !is_remote_url(&plan.code_url) {\n                let p = PathBuf::from(&plan.code_url);\n                if !p.exists() {\n                    return Err(format!(\n                        \"--repo-remote: code path '{}' does not exist — \\\n                         pre-create with `git init --bare`, or use --repo-local for fixture creation\",\n                        p.display()\n                    )\n                    .into());\n                }\n            }\n            if let Some(session_url) = plan.session_url.as_ref()\n                && !is_remote_url(session_url)\n            {\n                let p = PathBuf::from(session_url);\n                if !p.exists() {\n                    return Err(format!(\n                        \"--repo-remote: session path '{}' does not exist — \\\n                         pre-create with `git init --bare`, or use --repo-local for fixture creation\",\n                        p.display()\n                    )\n                    .into());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let templates = match &args.use_template {\n        Some(s) => {\n            let (code_t, bot_t) = parse_use_template(s)?;\n            if is_dual {\n                validate_templates(&code_t, &bot_t)?;\n                Some((code_t, Some(bot_t)))\n            } else {\n                validate_template_one(\"code\", &code_t)?;\n                Some((code_t, None))\n            }\n        }\n        None => None,\n    };\n\n    let visibility = if args.private {\n        \"--private\"\n    } else {\n        \"--public\"\n    };\n\n    if args.dry_run {\n        info!(\"Dry run — would execute:\");\n        info!(\"  1. Create directories: {}\", plan.project_dir.display());\n        info!(\n            \"  2. git init + jj git init --colocate on {}\",\n            if is_dual { \"both repos\" } else { \"the code repo\" }\n        );\n        info!(\n            \"  3. (skipped — no cross-reference in single-repo)\");\n            info!(\"  7. // Step 4: Copy templates (if --use-template)\n    if let Some((code_t, bot_t)) = &templates {\n        info!(\"Step 4: Copying templates...\");\n        info!(\n            \"  code: {} -> {}\",\n            code_t.display(),\n            plan.project_dir.display()\n        );\n        copy_template_recursive(code_t, &plan.project_dir)?;\n        rewrite_readme_first_line(&plan.project_dir, &plan.name)?;\n        if let (Some(bot_t), Some(session_dir), Some(session_name)) = (\n            bot_t.as_ref(),\n            plan.session_dir.as_ref(),\n            plan.session_name.as_ref(),\n        ) {\n            info!(\"  bot:  {} -> {}\", bot_t.display(), session_dir.display());\n            copy_template_recursive(bot_t, session_dir)?;\n            rewrite_readme_first_line(session_dir, session_name)?;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Step 5: jj commit\n    if is_dual {\n        info!(\"Step 5: Committing both repos with placeholder ochids...\");\n        #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n        let session_dir = plan.session_dir.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: is_dual ⇒ session_dir set\n        debug!(\"code side: initial commit with placeholder ochid (rewritten in step 6)\");\n        run(\n            \"jj\",\n            &[\"commit\", \"-m\", \"Initial commit\\n\\nochid: /none\"],\n            &plan.project_dir,\n        )?;\n        debug!(\"session side: initial commit with placeholder ochid (rewritten in step 6)\");\n        run(\n            \"jj\",\n            &[\"commit\", \"-m\", \"Initial commit\\n\\nochid: /none\"],\n            session_dir,\n        )?;\n    } else {\n        info!(\"Step 5: Committing code...\");\n        debug!(\"code side: initial commit (no ochid in single-repo)\");\n        run(\"jj\", &[\"commit\", \"-m\", \"Initial commit\"], &plan.project_dir)?;\n    }\n\n    // Step 6: ochid cross-references (dual only)\n    if is_dual {\n        info!(\"Step 6: Setting ochid cross-references...\");\n        #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n        let session_dir = plan.session_dir.as_ref().unwrap(); // OK: is_dual ⇒ session_dir set\n        let code_chid = jj_chid(\"@-\", &plan.project_dir)?;\n        let session_chid = jj_chid(\"@-\", session_dir)?;\n\n        let code_desc = format!(\"Initial commit\\n\\nochid: /.claude/{session_chid}\");\n        let session_desc = format!(\"Initial commit\\n\\nochid: /{code_chid}\");\n        debug!(\"code side: rewrite initial commit's ochid to point at session chid\");\n        run(\n            \"jj\",\n            &[\"describe\", \"@-\", \"-m\", &code_desc],\n            &plan.project_dir,\n        )?;\n        debug!(\"session side: rewrite initial commit's ochid to point at code chid\");\n        run(\"jj\", &[\"describe\", \"@-\", \"-m\", &session_desc], session_dir)?;\n\n        debug!(\"surface post-describe git hashes for the debug log\");\n        let hash = run(\"git\", &[\"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"], &plan.project_dir)?;\n        debug!(\"code repo: chid={code_chid} hash={hash}\");\n        let hash = run(\"git\", &[\"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"], session_dir)?;\n        debug!(\".claude:   chid={session_chid} hash={hash}\");\n    } else {\n        info!(\"Step 6: (skipped — no cross-reference in single-repo)\");\n        let code_chid = jj_chid(\"@-\", &plan.project_dir)?;\n        let hash = run(\"git\", &[\"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"], &plan.project_dir)?;\n        debug!(\"code repo: chid={code_chid} hash={hash}\");\n    }\n\n    // Step 7: Set bookmarks (creates git branches), then remove jj\n    info!(\"Step 7: Setting bookmarks and removing jj...\");\n    debug!(\"place code-side main bookmark at the initial commit\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"bookmark\", \"set\", \"main\", \"-r\", \"@-\"],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    if let Some(session_dir) = plan.session_dir.as_ref() {\n        debug!(\"place session-side main bookmark at the initial commit\");\n        run(\"jj\", &[\"bookmark\", \"set\", \"main\", \"-r\", \"@-\"], session_dir)?;\n        debug!(\"strip jj state from session side before its git push\");\n        run(\"git\", &[\"clean\", \"-xdf\"], session_dir)?;\n        debug!(\"strip jj state from code side, preserving nested .claude/\");\n        run(\n            \"git\",\n            &[\"clean\", \"-xdf\", \"--exclude\", \".claude\"],\n            &plan.project_dir,\n        )?;\n        debug!(\"re-attach session HEAD to main after clean\");\n        run(\"git\", &[\"checkout\", \"main\"], session_dir)?;\n    } else {\n        debug!(\"strip jj state from code side (no .claude in single-repo)\");\n        run(\"git\", &[\"clean\", \"-xdf\"], &plan.project_dir)?;\n    }\n    debug!(\"re-attach code HEAD to main after clean\");\n    run(\"git\", &[\"checkout\", \"main\"], &plan.project_dir)?;\n\n    // Steps 8/9: provision remotes and push.\n    if let Some(session_dir) = plan.session_dir.as_ref() {\n        #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n        let session_url = plan.session_url.as_deref().unwrap(); // OK: is_dual ⇒ session_url set\n        run_remote_step(\n            \"Step 8\",\n            \"session\",\n            &plan,\n            session_url,\n            plan.gh_session_slug.as_deref(),\n            plan.session_bare_path.as_deref(),\n            visibility,\n            session_dir,\n            args,\n        )?;\n    } else {\n        info!(\"Step 8: (skipped — no session side in single-repo)\");\n    }\n    run_remote_step(\n        \"Step 9\",\n        \"code\",\n        &plan,\n        &plan.code_url,\n        plan.gh_code_slug.as_deref(),\n        plan.code_bare_path.as_deref(),\n        visibility,\n        &plan.project_dir,\n        args,\n    )?;\n\n    // Step 10: Re-initialize jj\n    info!(\n        \"Step 10: Re-initializing jj on {}...\",\n        if is_dual { \"both repos\" } else { \"the code repo\" }\n    );\n    debug!(\"re-colocate jj atop the now-remote-linked code repo\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"--quiet\", \"git\", \"init\", \"--colocate\"],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"restore code-side main bookmark after re-init\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"bookmark\", \"set\", \"main\", \"-r\", \"@-\"],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    debug!(\"enable jj tracking of code-side main against origin\");\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"bookmark\", \"track\", \"main\", \"--remote=origin\"],\n        &plan.project_dir,\n    )?;\n    crate::common::verify_tracking(&plan.project_dir, \"main\")?;\n    let code_chid_final = jj_chid(\"@-\", &plan.project_dir)?;\n\n    let session_chid_final = if let Some(session_dir) = plan.session_dir.as_ref() {\n        debug!(\"re-colocate jj atop the now-remote-linked session repo\");\n        run(\"jj\", &[\"--quiet\", \"git\", \"init\", \"--colocate\"], session_dir)?;\n        debug!(\"restore session-side main bookmark after re-init\");\n        run(\"jj\", &[\"bookmark\", \"set\", \"main\", \"-r\", \"@-\"], session_dir)?;\n        debug!(\"enable jj tracking of session-side main against origin\");\n        run(\n            \"jj\",\n            &[\"bookmark\", \"track\", \"main\", \"--remote=origin\"],\n            session_dir,\n        )?;\n        crate::common::verify_tracking(session_dir, \"main\")?;\n        Some(jj_chid(\"@-\", session_dir)?)\n    } else {\n        None\n    };\n\n    // Step 11: Create Claude Code symlink (dual only)\n    let sl_opt = if is_dual {\n        info!(\"Step 11: Creating Claude Code symlink...\");\n        let symlink_dir = {\n            let home = std::env::var(\"HOME\")\n                .map_err(|_| \"HOME environment variable not set\".to_string())?;\n            PathBuf::from(home).join(\".claude\").join(\"projects\")\n        };\n\n        let sl = symlink::SymLink::new(&plan.project_dir, Path::new(\".claude\"), &symlink_dir)?;\n        sl.create(false)?;\n        Some(sl)\n    } else {\n        info!(\"Step 11: (skipped — no .claude symlink in single-repo)\");\n        None\n    };\n\n    info!(\"\");\n    info!(\"Done! Project created at {}\", plan.project_dir.display());\n    info!(\n        \"  Code repo:    {}  (chid={code_chid_final})\",\n        plan.code_url\n    );\n    if let (Some(session_url), Some(session_chid)) =\n        (plan.session_url.as_ref(), session_chid_final.as_ref())\n    {\n        info!(\"  Session repo: {session_url}  (chid={session_chid})\");\n    }\n    if let Some(sl) = sl_opt.as_ref() {\n        info!(\n            \"  Symlink:      {} -> {}\",\n            sl.symlink_path.display(),\n            sl.abs_target.display()\n        );\n    }\n\n    debug!(\"init: exit\");\n    Ok(())\n}"
}

Some(\"/tmp/xyz\"),\n            None,\n            None,\n            None,\n            false,\n            Some(vec![Side::Code]),\n        );\n        let plan = plan_init(&args).unwrap();\n        assert!(plan.scope.is_code_only());\n        assert_eq!(plan.provisioner, Provisioner::LocalBareInit);\n        assert_eq!(plan.project_dir, PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/xyz/tf1\"));\n        assert_eq!(\n            plan.code_bare_path,\n            Some(PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/xyz/remote.git\"))\n        );\n        assert_eq!(plan.code_url, \"/tmp/xyz/remote.git\");\n        assert!(plan.session_bare_path.is_none());\n        assert!(plan.session_url.is_none());\n        assert!(plan.session_dir.is_none());\n        assert!(plan.session_name.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn scope_code_only_repo_remote_github() {\n        let args = fixture_scoped(\n            Some(\"tf1\"),\n            None,\n            Some(\"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1\"),\n            None,\n            None,\n            false,\n            Some(vec![Side::Code]),\n        );\n        let plan = plan_init(&args).unwrap();\n        assert!(plan.scope.is_code_only());\n        assert_eq!(plan.provisioner, Provisioner::GhCreate);\n        assert_eq!(plan.code_url, \"git@github.com:winksaville/tf1.git\");\n        assert_eq!(plan.gh_code_slug.as_deref(), Some(\"winksaville/tf1\"));\n        assert!(plan.session_url.is_none());\n        assert!(plan.gh_session_slug.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn scope_code_only_repo_remote_non_github_skips_create() {\n        let args = fixture_scoped(\n            Some(\"tf1\"),\n            None,\n            Some(\"git@gitlab.com:winksaville/tf1.git\"),\n            None,\n            None,\n            false,\n            Some(vec![Side::Code]),\n        );\n        let plan = plan_init(&args).unwrap();\n        assert!(plan.scope.is_code_only());\n        assert_eq!(plan.provisioner, Provisioner::ExternalPreExisting);\n        assert!(plan.session_url.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn scope_code_only_with_comma_template_errors() {\n        // `--scope=code` + `--use-template foo,bar` is ambiguous — the\n        // bot half has no home.\n        let mut args = fixture_scoped(\n            Some(\"tf1\"),\n            Some(\"/tmp/xyz\"),\n            None,\n            None,\n            None,\n            false,\n            Some(vec![Side::Code]),\n        );\n        args.use_template = Some(\"/tmp/code,/tmp/bot\".to_string());\n        let err = plan_init(&args).unwrap_err().to_string();\n        assert!(err.contains(\"--scope=code\"));\n        assert!(err.contains(\"single template path\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn config_content_app_only() {\n        assert!(VC_CONFIG_APP_ONLY.contains(\"path = \\\"/\\\"\"));\n        assert!(!VC_CONFIG_APP_ONLY.contains(\"other-repo\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn gitignore_app_only_omits_claude() {\n        assert!(!GITIGNORE_APP_ONLY.contains(\"/.claude\"));\n        assert!(GITIGNORE_APP_ONLY.contains(\"/.git\"));\n        assert!(GITIGNORE_APP_ONLY.contains(\"/.jj\"));\n        assert!(GITIGNORE_APP_ONLY.contains(\"/.vc-x1\"));\n    }\n}"
}

vc-x1 0.40.0-2

Create a new dual-repo project

Usage: vc-x1 init [OPTIONS] [NAME]

Arguments:
  [NAME]
          Project name (directory and GitHub repo name).
          
          - Optional with `--repo-remote` (derived from URL's last
            path segment, trailing `.git` stripped).
          - Required with `--repo-local` and in default mode.
          - Fatal if both given and they disagree.

Options:
      --owner <OWNER>
          GitHub user or organization.
          
          - Only meaningful in default mode.
          - Fatal with `--repo-local` or `--repo-remote`.

  -v, --verbose...
          Verbose output: -v debug, -vv trace

      --dir <DIR>
          Parent directory to create the project under [default: cwd].
          
          - Fatal with `--repo-local` (that flag supplies the parent).

      --log <LOG>
          Log file path (captures all levels)

      --private
          Create private GitHub repos (default: public).
          
          - Only meaningful in default mode.
          - Fatal with `--repo-local` or `--repo-remote`.

      --dry-run
          Dry run — show what would be done without executing

      --push-retries <PUSH_RETRIES>
          Max push retries after repo creation [default: 5]
          
          [default: 5]

      --push-retry-delay <PUSH_RETRY_DELAY>
          Seconds between push retries [default: 3]
          
          [default: 3]

      --use-template <CODE[,BOT]>
          Seed both repos from template directories.
          
          Value is `CODE[,BOT]`. If `BOT` is omitted, defaults to the sibling
          directory `<CODE>.claude` (file-name concat, not path join — they are
          not nested). Non-hidden contents are copied recursively; hidden
          entries (names starting with `.`) are skipped since init creates the
          repo's own hidden files. If either template has a `README.md`, its
          first line is rewritten to `# <repo-name>`.

      --repo-local <PARENT>
          Fixture mode — fully self-contained local workspace.
          
          Value is a single parent directory.
          
          - Project + session at `<PARENT>/<NAME>/` and
            `<PARENT>/<NAME>/.claude/`.
          - Bare origins at `<PARENT>/remote-code.git` and
            `<PARENT>/remote-claude.git`.
          - No `gh`, no network.
          - Supports `~/…`, `$VAR`, `${VAR}`; relative paths against cwd.
          - Exclusive with `--repo-remote`, `--dir`, `--owner`, `--private`.

      --repo-remote <URL>
          Override the `origin` URL stem (single value).
          
          - Session URL derived: insert `.claude` before `.git` suffix,
            or append if no `.git`.
```

813	**Scope default resolution** (no `--scope` given):
814	
815	- No `.vc-config.toml` → `--scope=app`.
816	- `.vc-config.toml` with `other-repo` missing/empty → `--scope=app`.
817	- `.vc-config.toml` with a non-empty `other-repo` → `--scope=both`.
818	
819	**Ambiguity rule.** Any ambiguous combination of scope-related
820	flags on one invocation is a fatal error — callers pick exactly
821	one way to express repo selection per call.
822	
823	**Cycle steps.**
824	
825	- **0.40.0-0** — this plan. Notes + version bump only.
826	- **0.40.0-1** — `vc-x1 init --repo-local` + `--repo-remote`.
827	  - Decouple init from `gh repo create`; unblock testable
828	    fixture-mode workflows and non-GitHub remotes.
829	  - **Ordering rationale (2026-04-24):** doing `--scope=app`
830	    first would build on a still-GitHub-coupled init and
831	    immediately need rework; flipping the order means `-2`
832	    lands cleanly on a remote-agnostic flow.
833	- **0.40.0-2** — `vc-x1 init --scope=app|other|both`.
834	  - `app` → single-repo workspace (no `.claude` subdir; one
835	    repo; no `ochid:` trailer on the initial commit).
836	  - `both` (default) → current dual-repo behavior.
837	  - `other` → fatal; meaningless at init time.
838	  - Other subcommands error if `--scope` is passed.
839	- Subsequent `-N` steps wire `--scope` into sync, push,
840	  finalize, read-only commands. Specifics decided per step.
841	
842	**Helper placement** is deliberately open.
843	
844	- `common.rs`, `init.rs`, or `scope.rs` / `scope_helpers.rs`
845	  are all fine; refactor later once more call sites exist.
846	- Plan is subject to change; `-0` records best guess, not a
847	  contract.
848	
849	### 0.40.0-1: init remote decoupling
850	
851	Two new flags on `vc-x1 init` plus a unified
852	`Provisioner` dispatch that subsumes the existing default mode.
853	
854	**Flags.**
855	
856	- `--repo-local <PARENT>` — fixture mode. Creates
857	  `<PARENT>/<NAME>/`, `<PARENT>/<NAME>/.claude/`,
858	  `<PARENT>/remote-code.git`, `<PARENT>/remote-claude.git`.
859	  No `gh`, no network. Supports `~/…`, `$VAR`, `${VAR}`.
860	- `--repo-remote <URL>` — single URL stem. Session URL
861	  derived (insert `.claude` before trailing `.git`, else
862	  append `.claude`). Supports paths, `file:///…`, HTTPS,
863	  SSH (scp-like and `ssh://`), and `~/…`/`$VAR`.
864	
865	**Equivalence (winksaville case):** `vc-x1 init tf1`,
866	`vc-x1 init tf1 --owner winksaville`, and
867	`vc-x1 init --repo-remote git@github.com:winksaville/tf1`
868	all resolve to the same plan and execute identically.
869	
870	**Provisioner dispatch.** All three modes feed an `InitPlan`
871	with `provisioner ∈ {LocalBareInit, GhCreate,
872	ExternalPreExisting}`.
873	
874	- `LocalBareInit` — `--repo-local`. `git init --bare` on the
875	  two bare paths.
876	- `GhCreate` — default mode, or `--repo-remote` with a
877	  GitHub URL (host detection on the URL string). `gh repo
878	  create` for both sides.
879	- `ExternalPreExisting` — `--repo-remote` with a non-GitHub
880	  URL/path. No create step; caller pre-creates.
881	
882	**Ambiguity rejections (fatal).**
883	
884	- `--repo-local` ⊕ `--repo-remote`.
885	- `--repo-local` ⊕ `--dir` / `--owner` / `--private`.
886	- `--repo-remote` ⊕ `--owner` / `--private`.
887	- `NAME` and `--repo-remote` URL with disagreeing names.
888	- `--repo-local` without `NAME`.
889	
890	**NAME derivation.** `--repo-remote` URL's last path segment
891	(trailing `.git` stripped) is used when positional `NAME` is
892	absent. NAME stays optional at the clap layer; runtime
893	errors cover the missing-required cases.
894	
895	**Documentary `debug!` lines** — every `run()` call in `init`
896	has a `debug!(…)` immediately above it documenting intent
897	(while `common::run` already logs the action). New convention
898	(2026-04-24); applied to `init` here, retrofit later if useful.
899	
900	**Future steps unblocked.**
901	
902	- `-3` migrates sync/push integration tests from `test_fixture`
903	  to `init --repo-local`.
904	- `-4` retires `test_fixture` once no callers remain.
905	
906	- `Cargo.toml`: `0.40.0-0` → `0.40.0-1`.
907	- `src/init.rs`: new flags, `Provisioner` enum, `InitPlan`,
908	  `plan_init` dispatcher, `run_remote_step`, helpers
909	  (`expand_vars`, `is_remote_url`, `is_github_url`,
910	  `normalize_remote`, `normalize_local_parent`,
911	  `ensure_git_suffix`, `derive_session_url`,
```

**App repo**
- `Cargo.toml`: `0.40.0-1` → `0.40.0-2`.
- `Cargo.lock`: auto-updated to match.
- `src/scope.rs` (new): `Side` clap enum (`code`/`bot`) + `Scope` newtype with side-check accessors. 5 unit tests.
- `src/main.rs`: `mod scope;`.
- `src/init.rs`:
  - `InitArgs` gains `--scope <SIDES>` (comma-delimited, verbatim doc).
  - `InitPlan` session-side fields turned into `Option<_>`; `scope` field added.
  - `plan_init` resolves scope first (default = `code,bot`), rejects `--scope=bot` and `--scope=code` + comma-template.
  - `plan_local_fixture` / `plan_external_remote` / `plan_default_github` each emit single- or dual-repo plans depending on scope; single-repo `--repo-local` uses `<PARENT>/remote.git` (no `-code` suffix).
  - `validate_templates` factored into `validate_template_one(label, path)` so single-repo validates only the code side.
  - `VC_CONFIG_APP_ONLY` (no `other-repo`) + `GITIGNORE_APP_ONLY` (no `/.claude`).
  - `init()` body gates session-side work on `plan.session_dir.as_ref()` / `is_dual`. Step 6 (ochid cross-ref) and step 11 (symlink) skip in single-repo; step 5 drops the placeholder trailer; step 7 uses plain `git clean -xdf`.
  - 9 new tests (`scope_*` + app-only config/gitignore content). Existing assertions updated for `Option`-typed fields. All 249 tests pass.
- `notes/chores-06.md`: cycle-step vocabulary updated (`app|other|both` → `code|bot|code,bot`); new shipped `### 0.40.0-2` subsection.
- `notes/todo.md`: adds `0.40.0-1` and `0.40.0-2` to Done.

**Bot repo**: session activity only.

Create a new dual-repo project

Usage: vc-x1 init [OPTIONS] [NAME]

Arguments:

          Project name (directory and GitHub repo name).
          
          - Optional with `--repo-remote` (derived from URL's last

          - Required with `--repo-local` and in default mode.

Options:
      --owner <OWNER>
          GitHub user or organization.
          
          - Only meaningful in default mode.
          - Fatal with `--repo-local` or `--repo-remote`.

  -v, --verbose...
          Verbose output: -v debug, -vv trace

      --dir <DIR>
          Parent directory to create the project under [default: cwd].
          
          - Fatal with `--repo-local` (that flag supplies the parent).

      --log <LOG>
          Log file path (captures all levels)

      --private
          Create private GitHub repos (default: public).
          
          - Only meaningful in default mode.
          - Fatal with `--repo-local` or `--repo-remote`.

      --dry-run
          Dry run — show what would be done without executing

      --push-retries <PUSH_RETRIES>
          Max push retries after repo creation [default: 5]
          
          [default: 5]

      --push-retry-delay <PUSH_RETRY_DELAY>
          Seconds between push retries [default: 3]
          
          [default: 3]

      --use-template <CODE[,BOT]>
          Seed both repos from template directories.
          
          Value is `CODE[,BOT]`. If `BOT` is omitted, defaults to the sibling
          directory `<CODE>.claude` (file-name concat, not path join — they are
          not nested). Non-hidden contents are copied recursively; hidden
          entries (names starting with `.`) are skipped since init creates the
          repo's own hidden files. If either template has a `README.md`, its
          first line is rewritten to `# <repo-name>`.

      --repo-local <PARENT>
          Fixture mode — fully self-contained local workspace.
          
          Value is a single parent directory.
          
          - Project + session at `<PARENT>/<NAME>/` and
            `<PARENT>/<NAME>/.claude/`.
          - Bare origins at `<PARENT>/remote-code.git` and
            `<PARENT>/remote-claude.git`.
          - No `gh`, no network.
          - Supports `~/…`, `$VAR`, `${VAR}`; relative paths against cwd.
          - Exclusive with `--repo-remote`, `--dir`, `--owner`, `--private`.

      --repo-remote <URL>
          Override the `origin` URL stem (single value).
          
          - Session URL derived: insert `.claude` before `.git` suffix,
            or append if no `.git`.
          - GitHub URLs → `gh repo create` (same as default).
          - Other URLs → caller pre-creates the remote; init only pushes.
          - Supports `~/…`, `$VAR`, `${VAR}` in path-shaped values.
          - Exclusive with `--repo-local`, `--owner`, `--private`.

      --scope <SIDES>
          Which side(s) of the workspace to create.
          
          - `code` — single-repo workspace, no `.claude/` session.
          - `bot` — fatal at init time (meaningless without code).
          - `code,bot` (default) — dual-repo workspace.
          
          [possible values: code, bot]

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

  -V, --version
          Print version

```
1	//! Shared test helpers for dual-repo integration tests.
2	//!
3	//! Provides a `Fixture` built on top of `crate::test_fixture::test_fixture`
4	//! plus a per-process unique-tempdir helper so parallel tests don't
5	//! collide. Lifted out of the `sync` test module (originally inline at
6	//! `sync.rs:521–560`) so `push`'s tests (0.37.0) and any future
7	//! subcommand's tests can sit on the same harness without
8	//! copy-paste drift.
9	//!
10	//! Test-only — the whole module is gated at its declaration site via
11	//! `#[cfg(test)] mod test_helpers;`.
12	
13	use std::fs;
14	use std::path::PathBuf;
15	use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
16	use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
17	
18	use crate::test_fixture::{TestFixtureArgs, test_fixture};
19	
20	/// Per-process counter so same-nanosecond tempdir collisions yield
21	/// distinct paths when tests run in parallel.
22	static COUNTER: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
23	
24	/// Build a unique tempdir path for a test fixture.
25	///
26	/// Combines a nanosecond timestamp with a per-process atomic counter
27	/// so parallel tests and same-nanosecond collisions both yield
28	/// distinct paths. Prefix is `vc-x1-test-<tag>-<ts>-<n>` so callers
29	/// from different subcommand tests stay discoverable in `$TMPDIR`.
30	pub fn unique_base(tag: &str) -> PathBuf {
31	    let ts = SystemTime::now()
32	        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
33	        .map(|d| d.as_nanos())
34	        .unwrap_or(0); // OK: clock error → 0 is harmless for unique tempdir naming
35	    let n = COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
36	    std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("vc-x1-test-{tag}-{ts}-{n}"))
37	}
38	
39	/// Owned dual-repo fixture with RAII cleanup.
40	///
41	/// Builds a fresh throwaway workspace under a unique tempdir via
42	/// `test_fixture`, exposing the two repo paths (`work` and
43	/// `work/.claude`). The tempdir tree is removed when the value is
44	/// dropped, so a panicking test still cleans up after itself.
45	pub struct Fixture {
46	    /// Root tempdir that owns both repos and their bare-git remotes.
47	    pub base: PathBuf,
48	    /// Code repo path (`<base>/work`).
49	    pub work: PathBuf,
50	    /// Bot session repo path (`<base>/work/.claude`).
51	    pub claude: PathBuf,
52	}
53	
54	impl Fixture {
55	    /// Build a fresh fixture in a unique tempdir.
56	    pub fn new(tag: &str) -> Self {
57	        let base = unique_base(tag);
58	        let args = TestFixtureArgs {
59	            path: Some(base.clone()),
60	            with_pending: false,
61	            use_template: None,
62	        };
63	        test_fixture(&args).expect("build test fixture");
64	        let work = base.join("work");
65	        let claude = work.join(".claude");
66	        Fixture { base, work, claude }
67	    }
68	
69	    /// Convenience: return both repo paths as a `Vec<PathBuf>`
70	    /// suitable for `sync_repos` (or any other `&[PathBuf]` API).
71	    pub fn repos(&self) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
72	        vec![self.work.clone(), self.claude.clone()]
73	    }
74	}
75	
76	impl Drop for Fixture {
77	    /// Remove the fixture tree on drop. Best-effort; a failure here
78	    /// doesn't fail the test.
79	    fn drop(&mut self) {
80	        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.base);
81	    }
82	}
83	
```

> AGENT

**Plan for 0.40.0-3: migrate `Fixture::new` off `test_fixture`**

The integration-test workspaces are all built through exactly one bottleneck — `Fixture::new` in `src/test_helpers.rs` (visible above). Rewriting that single function to call `init::init` with `--repo-local <base>` and `NAME=work` swaps the harness over in one shot; the `work` / `claude` paths it exposes stay identical, so sync/push integration tests need no change.

Main things to watch for:
- `test_fixture` supports `with_pending: bool` and `use_template: Option<String>`. `with_pending` is used by some sync tests to seed a dirty working copy — I'll need to replicate that post-init (jj describe/write a placeholder file). `use_template` maps straight to `InitArgs.use_template`.
- `init::init` emits `info!()` lines that'll clutter test output. Log level during tests is typically `Error`, so this should stay quiet in practice; I'll verify.
- `init::init` also does `gh` / `jj` PATH checks. `gh` is skipped under `LocalBareInit`; `jj` is already required by the existing tests.

**Proposal:** single-step bump to `0.40.0-3` (one commit). Scope is mechanical and contained; no need for sub-steps.

OK to proceed with that plan?

> DEVELOPER

y

12	/// Create a throwaway dual-repo jj fixture for testing.
13	///
14	/// Mirrors the real dual-repo layout created by `vc-x1 init` minus the
15	/// GitHub side and the `~/.claude/projects/` symlink: app repo at
16	/// `<base>/work/`, bot session repo at `<base>/work/.claude/`, and a local
17	/// bare-git remote per repo under `<base>/remote-*.git/`. Both repos carry
18	/// `.vc-config.toml`, `.gitignore`, an initial described commit with
19	/// matching `ochid:` trailers, and a tracked `main` bookmark.
20	///
21	/// The remotes are local bare-git directories inside the fixture — no
22	/// network, no GitHub. See README.md § test-fixture for details.
23	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
24	pub struct TestFixtureArgs {
25	    /// Fixture root directory [default: $TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>]
26	    #[arg(long, value_name = "PATH")]
27	    pub path: Option<PathBuf>,
28	
29	    /// Leave uncommitted changes in @ so finalize --squash has real work
30	    #[arg(long)]
31	    pub with_pending: bool,
32	
33	    /// Seed both repos from template directories.
34	    ///
35	    /// Same format and semantics as `vc-x1 init --use-template`:
36	    /// `CODE[,BOT]`. If `BOT` is omitted, defaults to the sibling directory
37	    /// `<CODE>.claude`. Non-hidden contents are copied recursively; hidden
38	    /// entries are skipped. `README.md` at the root of each target (if
39	    /// present) gets its first line rewritten to `# <repo-name>`, where
40	    /// the code repo is `work` and the bot repo is `work.claude`.
41	    #[arg(long, value_name = "CODE[,BOT]")]
42	    pub use_template: Option<String>,
43	}
44	
45	/// Remove a test fixture. Refuses anything whose directory name doesn't
46	/// start with `vc-x1-test-`, so it cannot chew up arbitrary paths.
47	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
48	pub struct TestFixtureRmArgs {
49	    /// Fixture root directory to remove
50	    #[arg(value_name = "PATH")]
51	    pub path: PathBuf,
52	}
53	
54	const VC_CONFIG_CODE: &str = r#"# vc-config: Vibe Coding workspace configuration
55	[workspace]
56	path = "/"
57	other-repo = ".claude"
58	"#;
59	
60	const VC_CONFIG_SESSION: &str = r#"# vc-config: Vibe Coding workspace configuration
61	[workspace]
62	path = "/.claude"
63	other-repo = ".."
64	"#;
65	
66	const GITIGNORE_CODE: &str = "/target
67	/.claude
68	/.git
69	/.jj
70	/.vc-x1
71	";
72	
73	const GITIGNORE_SESSION: &str = ".git
74	.jj
75	";
76	
77	fn jj_chid(rev: &str, cwd: &Path) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
78	    let full = run(
79	        "jj",
80	        &[
81	            "log",
82	            "-r",
83	            rev,
84	            "--no-graph",
85	            "-T",
86	            "change_id",
87	            "--limit",
88	            "1",
89	        ],
90	        cwd,
91	    )?;
92	    Ok(full[..full.len().min(12)].to_string())
93	}

95	pub fn test_fixture(args: &TestFixtureArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
96	    debug!("test_fixture: enter");
97	
98	    let base = match &args.path {
99	        Some(p) => p.clone(),
100	        None => {
101	            let ts = SystemTime::now()
102	                .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
103	                .map_err(|e| format!("time error: {e}"))?
104	                .as_nanos();
105	            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("vc-x1-test-{ts}"))
106	        }
107	    };
108	
109	    if base.exists() {
110	        return Err(format!("'{}' already exists", base.display()).into());
111	    }
112	
113	    let templates = match &args.use_template {
114	        Some(s) => {
115	            let (code_t, bot_t) = parse_use_template(s)?;
116	            validate_templates(&code_t, &bot_t)?;
117	            Some((code_t, bot_t))
118	        }
119	        None => None,
120	    };
121	
122	    info!("Creating test fixture at {}", base.display());
123	
124	    let remote_code = base.join("remote-code.git");
125	    let remote_claude = base.join("remote-claude.git");
126	    let work_dir = base.join("work");
127	    let session_dir = work_dir.join(".claude");
128	
129	    mkdir_p(&base)?;
130	    mkdir_p(&remote_code)?;
131	    mkdir_p(&remote_claude)?;
132	    mkdir_p(&work_dir)?;
133	    mkdir_p(&session_dir)?;
134	
135	    info!("Step 1: Initializing bare git remotes...");
136	    run("git", &["init", "--bare"], &remote_code)?;
137	    run("git", &["init", "--bare"], &remote_claude)?;
138	
139	    info!("Step 2: Initializing work repo (jj colocated)...");
140	    run("jj", &["git", "init", "--colocate"], &work_dir)?;
141	    write_file(&work_dir.join(".vc-config.toml"), VC_CONFIG_CODE)?;
142	    write_file(&work_dir.join(".gitignore"), GITIGNORE_CODE)?;
143	
144	    info!("Step 3: Initializing .claude session repo (jj colocated)...");
145	    run("jj", &["git", "init", "--colocate"], &session_dir)?;
146	    write_file(&session_dir.join(".vc-config.toml"), VC_CONFIG_SESSION)?;
147	    write_file(&session_dir.join(".gitignore"), GITIGNORE_SESSION)?;
148	
149	    if let Some((code_t, bot_t)) = &templates {
150	        info!("Step 3b: Copying templates...");
151	        info!("  code: {} -> {}", code_t.display(), work_dir.display());
152	        copy_template_recursive(code_t, &work_dir)?;
153	        rewrite_readme_first_line(&work_dir, "work")?;
154	        info!("  bot:  {} -> {}", bot_t.display(), session_dir.display());
155	        copy_template_recursive(bot_t, &session_dir)?;
156	        rewrite_readme_first_line(&session_dir, "work.claude")?;
157	    }
158	
159	    info!("Step 4: Initial commits with placeholder ochids...");
160	    run(
161	        "jj",
162	        &["commit", "-m", "initial commit\n\nochid: /none"],
163	        &work_dir,
164	    )?;
165	    run(
166	        "jj",
167	        &["commit", "-m", "initial commit\n\nochid: /none"],
168	        &session_dir,
169	    )?;
170	
171	    info!("Step 5: Setting ochid cross-references...");
172	    let code_chid = jj_chid("@-", &work_dir)?;
173	    let session_chid = jj_chid("@-", &session_dir)?;
174	    let code_desc = format!("initial commit\n\nochid: /.claude/{session_chid}");
175	    let session_desc = format!("initial commit\n\nochid: /{code_chid}");
176	    run("jj", &["describe", "@-", "-m", &code_desc], &work_dir)?;
177	    run("jj", &["describe", "@-", "-m", &session_desc], &session_dir)?;
178	
179	    info!("Step 6: Setting bookmarks and wiring remotes...");
180	    run("jj", &["bookmark", "set", "main", "-r", "@-"], &work_dir)?;
181	    run("jj", &["bookmark", "set", "main", "-r", "@-"], &session_dir)?;
182	    let code_url = remote_code.canonicalize()?.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
183	    let claude_url = remote_claude.canonicalize()?.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
184	    run("git", &["remote", "add", "origin", &code_url], &work_dir)?;
185	    run(
186	        "git",
187	        &["remote", "add", "origin", &claude_url],
188	        &session_dir,
189	    )?;
190	
191	    info!("Step 7: Pushing main to both remotes...");
192	    run("jj", &["git", "push", "--bookmark", "main"], &work_dir)?;
193	    crate::common::verify_tracking(&work_dir, "main")?;
194	    run("jj", &["git", "push", "--bookmark", "main"], &session_dir)?;
195	    crate::common::verify_tracking(&session_dir, "main")?;
196	
197	    if args.with_pending {
198	        info!("Step 8: Writing uncommitted changes to @ (both repos)...");
199	        write_file(&work_dir.join("TODO.md"), "# TODO\n- first feature\n")?;
200	        write_file(
201	            &session_dir.join("session-notes.md"),
202	            "# Session notes\n- simulated pending work\n",
203	        )?;
204	    }
205	
206	    info!("");
207	    info!("Fixture ready (local bare-git remotes, see README.md § test-fixture):");
208	    info!("  Code repo:     {}", work_dir.display());
209	    info!("  Session repo:  {}", session_dir.display());
210	    info!("  Code remote:   {}", remote_code.display());
211	    info!("  Claude remote: {}", remote_claude.display());
212	    info!("");
213	    info!("Next steps — see README.md § Testing push + finalize for the full flow.");
214	    info!("Quick reference with this fixture's paths:");
215	    info!("  jj git push -R {}", work_dir.display());
216	    info!(
217	        "  vc-x1 finalize --repo {} --squash --push main --detach",
218	        session_dir.display()
219	    );
220	    info!("  vc-x1 test-fixture-rm {}", base.display());
221	
222	    debug!("test_fixture: exit");
223	    Ok(())
224	}
225	
226	pub fn test_fixture_rm(args: &TestFixtureRmArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
227	    debug!("test_fixture_rm: enter args={args:?}");
228	    let path = args
229	        .path
230	        .canonicalize()
231	        .map_err(|e| format!("cannot resolve path '{}': {e}", args.path.display()))?;
232	    let name = path
233	        .file_name()
234	        .ok_or_else(|| format!("path '{}' has no final component", path.display()))?
235	        .to_string_lossy()
236	        .into_owned();
237	    if !name.starts_with("vc-x1-test-") {
238	        return Err(format!(
239	            "refusing to remove '{}': directory name '{name}' does not start with 'vc-x1-test-'",
240	            path.display()
241	        )
242	        .into());
243	    }
244	    info!("Removing fixture at {}", path.display());
245	    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&path)?;
246	    info!("Done.");
247	    debug!("test_fixture_rm: exit");
248	    Ok(())
249	}

258	        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(args).unwrap();
259	        match cli.command {

262	        }
