{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/src/test_helpers.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Owned dual-repo fixture with RAII cleanup.\n///\n/// Builds a fresh throwaway workspace under a unique tempdir via\n/// `test_fixture`, exposing the two repo paths (`work` and\n/// `work/.claude`). The tempdir tree is removed when the value is\n/// dropped, so a panicking test still cleans up after itself.\npub struct Fixture {\n    /// Root tempdir that owns both repos and their bare-git remotes.\n    pub base: PathBuf,\n    /// Code repo path (`<base>/work`).\n    pub work: PathBuf,\n    /// Bot session repo path (`<base>/work/.claude`).\n    pub claude: PathBuf,\n}\n\nimpl Fixture {\n    /// Build a fresh fixture in a unique tempdir.\n    pub fn new(tag: &str) -> Self {\n        let base = unique_base(tag);\n        let args = TestFixtureArgs {\n            path: Some(base.clone()),\n            with_pending: false,\n            use_template: None,\n        };\n        test_fixture(&args).expect(\"build test fixture\");\n        let work = base.join(\"work\");\n        let claude = work.join(\".claude\");\n        Fixture { base, work, claude }\n    }\n\n    /// Convenience: return both repo paths as a `Vec<PathBuf>`\n    /// suitable for `sync_repos` (or any other `&[PathBuf]` API).\n    pub fn repos(&self) -> Vec<PathBuf> {\n        vec![self.work.clone(), self.claude.clone()]\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Owned dual-repo fixture with RAII cleanup.\n///\n/// Builds a fresh throwaway workspace under a unique tempdir by\n/// driving `init::init_with_symlink` in `--repo-local` mode with\n/// `create_symlink=false`, exposing the two repo paths (`work` and\n/// `work/.claude`). The tempdir tree is removed when the value is\n/// dropped, so a panicking test still cleans up after itself.\npub struct Fixture {\n    /// Root tempdir that owns both repos and their bare-git remotes.\n    pub base: PathBuf,\n    /// Code repo path (`<base>/work`).\n    pub work: PathBuf,\n    /// Bot session repo path (`<base>/work/.claude`).\n    pub claude: PathBuf,\n}\n\nimpl Fixture {\n    /// Build a fresh fixture in a unique tempdir.\n    pub fn new(tag: &str) -> Self {\n        Self::new_opts(tag, false, None)\n    }\n\n    /// Build a fresh fixture with optional pending changes and a\n    /// template seed.\n    ///\n    /// - `with_pending` — after init, write a small file into each\n    ///   repo so `@` carries uncommitted changes (useful for\n    ///   finalize / push tests).\n    /// - `use_template` — `CODE[,BOT]` forwarded to init.\n    pub fn new_opts(tag: &str, with_pending: bool, use_template: Option<String>) -> Self {\n        let base = unique_base(tag);\n        // init refuses to reuse an existing project_dir, so `base`\n        // must not exist yet. `unique_base` already guarantees that.\n        let args = InitArgs {\n            name: Some(\"work\".to_string()),\n            owner: None,\n            dir: None,\n            private: false,\n            dry_run: false,\n            push_retries: 5,\n            push_retry_delay: 3,\n            use_template,\n            repo_local: Some(base.to_string_lossy().into_owned()),\n            repo_remote: None,\n            scope: None, // default → code,bot\n        };\n        init_with_symlink(&args, false).expect(\"build test fixture via init\");\n\n        let work = base.join(\"work\");\n        let claude = work.join(\".claude\");\n\n        if with_pending {\n            write_file(&work.join(\"TODO.md\"), \"# TODO\\n- first feature\\n\")\n                .expect(\"write pending TODO.md\");\n            write_file(\n                &claude.join(\"session-notes.md\"),\n                \"# Session notes\\n- simulated pending work\\n\",\n            )\n            .expect(\"write pending session-notes.md\");\n        }\n\n        Fixture { base, work, claude }\n    }\n\n    /// Convenience: return both repo paths as a `Vec<PathBuf>`\n    /// suitable for `sync_repos` (or any other `&[PathBuf]` API).\n    pub fn repos(&self) -> Vec<PathBuf> {\n        vec![self.work.clone(), self.claude.clone()]\n    }\n}"
}

Next up is **0.40.0-4: retire `test_fixture`**. Since `-3` removed the only programmatic caller of `test_fixture::test_fixture`, the module exists only to back the `vc-x1 test-fixture` / `vc-x1 test-fixture-rm` CLI subcommands. Plan:

- Delete `src/test_fixture.rs`.
- Drop `mod test_fixture;`, `Commands::TestFixture`, `Commands::TestFixtureRm`, and their dispatch arms from `src/main.rs`.
- Sweep README.md / CLAUDE.md for `test-fixture` references and remove or redirect to `init --repo-local`.

One open question for the cycle as a whole: the original `0.40.0` plan in `chores-06.md` mentioned wiring `--scope` into sync/push/finalize as later sub-steps. So far we've done init + test infrastructure only. Two reasonable options:

- **(a) End cycle here:** make `-4` test_fixture retirement, then a final `0.40.0` close-out commit. Defer sync/push/finalize `--scope` wiring to a follow-up cycle (`0.41.0` in chores-07).
- **(b) Continue cycle:** after `-4`, add `-5`+ to wire sync/push/finalize. Could grow large.

I'd lean (a) — this cycle has already shipped a foundational chunk and the `-3` test migration is a clean stopping point. The cycle title is "Generalize --scope across commands" which technically promises more, but renaming/rescoping the section is cheap.

Which way do you want to go for the cycle, and should `-4` be a single-step bump to `0.40.0-4`?

35	   Alternative to `--title` + `--body`. [58]
36	1. Mirror `--check` / `--no-check` onto `vc-x1 push` (forwards
37	   through to the preflight `vc-x1 sync` invocation).
38	   0.37.1 hard-codes `--check`; default stays `--check`.
39	1. Add `status` (alias `st`) subcommand: `jj st` across both
40	   repos in one shot. Uses `--scope` from day one. Natural
41	   home for the working-copy signal called out in [54].
42	1. Add `validate-repo` subcommand: diagnostic that runs all
43	   `verify_*` checks (tracking, push state freshness, ochid
44	   integrity, conflicts, config sanity, working-copy state)
45	   and reports per-check pass/fail. Exit code = number of
46	   failed checks. Implementation: promote
47	   `verify_state_sanity` / `verify_completion_sanity` from
48	   push.rs to `common.rs`. [69]
49	1. sync: surface working-copy state in the up-to-date summary
50	   (per-repo pending-files count or compact stat). Wording-only
51	   fix shipped in 0.37.1; this is the design+impl. [54]
52	1. bm-track silent-when-clean refinement. Print on entry/exit
53	   only when state isn't fully tracked or when exit state
54	   differs from entry. [62]
55	1. "Oh shit" revert — post-success undo via `.vc-x1-ops/`
56	   anchor dir. Idea-stage; every repo-mutating command drops a
57	   pre-op snapshot, `vc-x1 undo` restores both repos. [57]
58	1. vc-x1 test-fixture should refuse `--path` values that resolve
59	   inside the current workspace root (error or warn). Dogfood
60	   surfaced this: `--path ./tf-1` inside the repo let jj
61	   snapshot the fixture's bare-git remotes — 56-file noise blob.
62	1. Restructure templates: replace separate `vc-template-x1` +
63	   `vc-template-x1.claude` repos with a single `vc-template-x1`
64	   that has `.claude/` as a subdir (covers `LICENSE-*` etc. for
65	   both sides in one place). Updates to `vc-x1 init` / `clone`
66	   needed for the new layout.
67	1. Source-code design ref sweep + CLAUDE.md codification:
68	   adopt section-name + `blob/main/...` URL pattern for source
69	   code refs to designs; codify in CLAUDE.md alongside the
70	   existing markdown ref conventions. Sweep targets:
71	   src/push.rs lines 4, 121, 645, 1219. [68]
72	1. Richer bookmark enumeration: per-bookmark remote presence + tracking status [52]
73	1. Per-line/per-thread runtime log points (future, maybe) [36]
74	1. Add Windows symlink support via `std::os::windows::fs::symlink_dir` [37]
75	1. Add "::" revision syntax for jj compatibility
76	1. Add -p, --parents, -c, --children so parent and child counts can be asymmetric
77	1. Add integration tests in tests/ for subcommands using temp jj repos (tempfile crate)
78	1. Fix .claude repo history: dev0 through dev2 sessions squashed into wrong commit [4],[5]
79	1. Add `vc-x1 setup` subcommand: completions install, .claude repo init, symlink setup [27]
80	1. Add dynamic revision completion via `ArgValueCompleter` (jj doesn't complete revsets either) [28],[29]
81

  "new_string": "- `src/test_helpers.rs`: drops `test_fixture` dependency;\n  `Fixture::new` + new `Fixture::new_opts` call\n  `init_with_symlink`. Pending-changes path moves to the\n  caller side.\n- `notes/chores-06.md`: this subsection.\n\n### 0.40.0: cycle close-out\n\nCloses the 0.40.0 cycle at the \"init `--scope` foundation\"\nboundary. The original plan listed a `-4` (`test_fixture`\nretirement) plus subsequent `-N` steps for sync / push /\nfinalize wiring; both move to 0.41.0 ([71]) so this cycle\nships a coherent, smaller chunk.\n\n**What shipped (0.40.0).**\n\n- `init --repo-local` / `--repo-remote` — remote\n  decoupling (`-1`).\n- `init --scope=code|bot|code,bot` — single- and\n  dual-repo init from one entry (`-2`).\n- Integration tests on `init --repo-local`; init gains a\n  symlink opt-out (`-3`).\n\n**Deferred to 0.41.0.**\n\n- `test_fixture` module + CLI subcommands retirement.\n- `vc-x1 sync --scope`.\n- `vc-x1 push --scope` (incl. single-repo state machine).\n- `vc-x1 finalize --scope` (TBD whether useful — finalize\n  already takes `--repo`).\n- Default-scope resolution from `.vc-config.toml` for the\n  newly scope-aware commands.\n\n- `Cargo.toml`: `0.40.0-3` → `0.40.0`.\n- `notes/todo.md`: 0.40.0 done marker; 0.41.0 in progress.\n- `notes/chores-06.md`: this subsection + cycle-steps\n  amendment + new `--scope continuation (0.41.0)` section.\n\n## --scope continuation (0.41.0)\n\nContinues the foundation laid in 0.40.0. Wires `--scope`\ninto the remaining commands and retires the\nnow-orphan `test_fixture` module.\n\n**Cycle steps (initial sketch).**\n\n- **0.41.0-0** — this plan + version bump. Notes only.\n- **0.41.0-1** — retire `test_fixture` module and the\n  `vc-x1 test-fixture` / `test-fixture-rm` CLI\n  subcommands. Inherited from 0.40.0's deferred `-4`.\n  Mechanical: `Fixture::new` already sources from\n  `init::init_with_symlink`, so no programmatic callers\n  remain. README / CLAUDE.md sweep for references.\n- **0.41.0-2** — `vc-x1 sync --scope=…`. Sync currently\n  hard-codes `[\".\", \".claude\"]` as the default repo set;\n  scope drives that instead. Default-scope resolution\n  reads `.vc-config.toml > [workspace] other-repo`\n  (present + non-empty → `code,bot`, else `code`).\n- **0.41.0-3** — `vc-x1 push --scope=…`. Trickier: push's\n  state machine assumes dual. Likely splits into a\n  single-repo path (skip `commit-claude` + bookmark for\n  `.claude` + `finalize-claude`) plus a scope-aware\n  preflight summary.\n- **0.41.0-4** — `vc-x1 finalize --scope` review.\n  Finalize already takes `--repo`; check whether `--scope`\n  adds anything or if this step becomes a no-op /\n  documentation-only entry.\n- **0.41.0 (final)** — cycle close-out + dogfood\n  validation on a single-repo project (e.g. apply the\n  full sync→edit→push flow against a `--scope=code`\n  fixture).\n\n**Default-scope resolution.** The rule was sketched in\n[60] using `app|other|both` vocabulary; restating in the\nshipped vocabulary:\n\n- No `.vc-config.toml` → `--scope=code`.\n- `.vc-config.toml` with `other-repo` missing or empty\n  → `--scope=code`.\n- `.vc-config.toml` with non-empty `other-repo`\n  → `--scope=code,bot`.\n\n**Scope mismatch warnings.** When the resolved scope\ndisagrees with the working-copy state (e.g. `--scope=code`\nin a workspace whose `.vc-config.toml` says dual), emit a\nwarning so the user can confirm or back out — do not\nsilently proceed. Fatal vs warn is sub-step decision.\n\n**Plan is exploratory.** Sub-step boundaries can shift\nonce each command's call sites are seen. Push in\nparticular may want its own `-N`-internal state; allow\nthe cycle to lengthen if needed.\n\n# References"
}

```
1	# vc-x1
2	
3	- [Overview](#vc-x1)
4	- [Usage](#usage)
5	  - [Revision shortcuts](#revision-shortcuts)
6	  - [Shell completion](#shell-completion)
7	  - [validate-desc](#validate-desc)
8	  - [fix-desc](#fix-desc)
9	  - [clone](#clone)
10	  - [init](#init)
11	  - [symlink](#symlink)
12	  - [finalize](#finalize)
13	  - [test-fixture](#test-fixture)
14	  - [Testing push + finalize](#testing-push--finalize)
15	- [Cross-repo Linking with Git Trailers](#cross-repo-linking-with-git-trailers)
16	- [jj Tips for Git Users](#jj-tips-for-git-users)
17	- [Contributing](#contributing)
18	- [License](#license)
19	
20	This is experiment 1 to explore creating a Vibe Coding (vc) environment.
21	We will investigate ways of using the dual jj-git repo concept, explored
22	in [hw-jjg-bot](https://github.com/winksaville/hw-jjg-bot.git) to
23	initially make it easy to see how the code base evolved. This is
24	made possible by the fact that we have two repos one with the code
25	and one with the conversation with the bot.
26	
27	I've chosen the jj-git environment because jj provides the concept that
28	each commit has an immutable changeID as well as the mutable commitID
29	of git. The idea is that each commit made on repo A writes the
30	changeID in the commit message to repo B. Thus there is a cross reference
31	between the two repos and this will allow vc-x1 to show how the repo
32	evolved and the entity (bot or human) can more clearly understand **how** and
33	most importantly **why** the code evolved.
34	
35	The solution space is wide open, from trivial CLI, web or app based
36	(mobile/non-mobile). In addition, I could see this as an extension to
37	existing programming editors like vscode and zed or even creating our
38	own IDE for vc.
39	
40	See [Initial commit with dual jj-git repos](./notes/chores-01.md#initial-commit-with-dual-jj-git-repos)
41	for how the initial commit was created with the dual jj-git repos. After
42	doing so and I then created this README.md file.
43	
44	## Usage
45	
46	```
47	vc-x1 list [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # List commits in a jj repo
48	vc-x1 desc [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # Show full description of a commit
49	vc-x1 chid [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # Print changeID(s) for a revision
50	vc-x1 show [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # Show commit details and diff summary
51	vc-x1 validate-desc [OPTS]                 # Validate commit descriptions
52	vc-x1 fix-desc [OPTS]                     # Fix commit descriptions (dry-run default)
53	vc-x1 clone <REPO> [NAME] [OPTS]          # Clone a dual-repo project
54	vc-x1 init <NAME> [OPTS]                  # Create a new dual-repo project
55	vc-x1 symlink [TARGET] [OPTS]             # Create Claude Code project symlink
56	vc-x1 sync [OPTS]                          # Fetch + sync both repos to their remotes
57	vc-x1 finalize --bookmark <B> [OPTS]       # Squash working copy into target
58	vc-x1 test-fixture [--path PATH]           # Create throwaway jj repo + remote for testing
59	vc-x1 --version                            # Print version
60	vc-x1 --help                           # Print help
61	```
62	
63	**The `..` notation:** dots on the revision show which direction to
64	list commits:
65	
66	- `x..` — x at top, ancestors below (older commits)
67	- `..x` — descendants above (newer commits), x at bottom
68	- `..x..` — both directions, x in the middle
69	
70	COMMITS is the total number of commits to show (x is always included).
71	Without dots, `x` with a count defaults to `x..` (ancestors).
72	For `..x..`, the budget is split: ancestors get the extra on odd counts.
73	
74	```
75	vc-x1 list -r @              # just @ (1 commit)
76	vc-x1 list -r @.. -n 5       # @ + 4 ancestors (5 commits, @ at top)
77	vc-x1 list -r ..@ -n 3       # 2 descendants + @ (3 commits, @ at bottom)
78	vc-x1 list -r ..@.. -n 3     # 1 descendant, @, 1 ancestor (3 commits)
79	vc-x1 list -r ..@.. -n 4     # 1 descendant, @, 2 ancestors (4 commits)
80	```
```

340	| `-v, --verbose` | Verbose output (show retry details) |
341	
342	**`--use-template`**. Value is `CODE[,BOT]`. If `BOT` is omitted, defaults
343	to the sibling directory `<CODE>.claude` (file-name concat, not path
344	join — the two templates are not nested). Non-hidden contents are
345	copied recursively into each target; hidden entries (names starting
346	with `.`) are skipped since init creates the repo's own hidden files
347	(`.vc-config.toml`, `.gitignore`, `.git/`, `.jj/`). If either template
348	has a `README.md` at its root, its first line is rewritten to
349	`# <repo-name>` — `<name>` for the code repo and `<name>.claude` for
350	the session repo. The same flag is also available on `test-fixture`
351	for local verification without hitting GitHub.
352	
353	Requires `gh` (authenticated) and `jj` to be installed.
354	
355	### symlink
356	
357	Create or verify the Claude Code project symlink. Claude Code stores
358	session data in `~/.claude/projects/<encoded-path>/`. This command
359	creates a symlink from that location to the local `.claude` directory.
360	
361	```
362	# Create symlink for current project (default target: .claude)
363	vc-x1 symlink
364	
365	# Specify a different target
366	vc-x1 symlink /path/to/session-dir
367	
368	# Replace existing symlink without prompting
369	vc-x1 symlink -y
370	
371	# List contents after creation
372	vc-x1 symlink -l
373	```
374	
375	| Flag | Description |
376	|------|-------------|
377	| `--symlink-dir <PATH>` | Override symlink parent [default: ~/.claude/projects] |
378	| `-l, --list` | List contents of symlinked directory after creation |
379	| `-y, --yes` | Replace existing symlink without prompting |
```

560	
561	See [Add push subcommand (0.37.0)](./notes/chores-05.md#add-push-subcommand-0370)
562	for the full design and [per-step record](./notes/chores-05.md#per-step-record)
563	for what each `0.37.0-N` dev step shipped.
564	
565	### test-fixture
566	
567	Scaffold a throwaway dual-repo jj workspace + local bare-git remotes
568	for testing `finalize` (and other subcommands) without touching live
569	workspace repos. Mirrors the real `vc-x1 init` layout minus the GitHub
570	side and the `~/.claude/projects/` symlink. Both repos get a described
571	initial commit with matching `ochid:` trailers, a tracked `main`
572	bookmark, and a pushed remote — so `finalize --push` flows work
573	end-to-end on either side.
574	
575	**Local remotes, not GitHub.** Each `origin` points at a bare-git
576	directory alongside the work trees (`<base>/remote-code.git/`,
577	`<base>/remote-claude.git/`) — no network, no auth, no GitHub. Pushes
578	succeed against these local bare repos and stay inside the fixture,
579	so nothing leaks out and nothing needs cleanup on a remote service.
580	When you're done, `vc-x1 test-fixture-rm <base>` wipes the whole
581	thing.
582	
583	```bash
584	vc-x1 test-fixture                 # base = $TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>
585	vc-x1 test-fixture --path /tmp/t1  # explicit path
586	vc-x1 test-fixture --use-template ../vc-template-x1  # seed from sibling templates
587	```
588	
589	`--use-template` takes the same `CODE[,BOT]` value as `vc-x1 init`
590	(bot defaults to `<CODE>.claude` sibling). Non-hidden template contents
591	are copied into `work/` and `work/.claude/`, and each repo's
592	`README.md` first line is rewritten to `# work` / `# work.claude`.
593	This is the path for eyeballing the template-copy result without
594	hitting GitHub.
595	
596	Layout:
597	```
598	<base>/
599	  remote-code.git/     bare git remote for code repo
600	  remote-claude.git/   bare git remote for .claude session repo
601	  work/                code repo (jj colocated, main tracks origin)
602	    .vc-config.toml    path="/",       other-repo=".claude"
603	    .gitignore         /.claude /.git /.jj /target /.vc-x1
604	    .claude/           session repo (jj colocated, main tracks origin)
605	      .vc-config.toml  path="/.claude", other-repo=".."
606	      .gitignore       .git .jj
607	```
608	
609	Example — running `vc-x1 test-fixture` (the timestamp suffix varies):
610	```
611	$ vc-x1 test-fixture
612	Creating test fixture at /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8
613	Step 1: Initializing bare git remotes...
614	Step 2: Initializing work repo (jj colocated)...
615	Initialized repo in "."
616	Hint: Running `git clean -xdf` will remove `.jj/`!
617	Step 3: Initializing .claude session repo (jj colocated)...
618	Initialized repo in "."
619	Hint: Running `git clean -xdf` will remove `.jj/`!
620	Step 4: Initial commits with placeholder ochids...
621	Working copy  (@) now at: znkwzomz 0ce34d9b (empty) (no description set)
622	Parent commit (@-)      : xoknyroz 93ca19eb initial commit
623	Working copy  (@) now at: kmtpuzox f8c9b5af (empty) (no description set)
624	Parent commit (@-)      : mwutluxv 40438e05 initial commit
625	Step 5: Setting ochid cross-references...
626	Rebased 1 descendant commits
627	Step 6: Setting bookmarks and wiring remotes...
628	Created 1 bookmarks pointing to xoknyroz 46b9cd88 main | initial commit
629	Created 1 bookmarks pointing to mwutluxv 31a49010 main | initial commit
630	Step 7: Pushing main to both remotes...
631	Changes to push to origin:
632	  Add bookmark main to 46b9cd886e64
633	Changes to push to origin:
634	  Add bookmark main to 31a490102db7
635	
636	Fixture ready (local bare-git remotes, see README.md § test-fixture):
637	  Code repo:     /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/work
638	  Session repo:  /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/work/.claude
639	  Code remote:   /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/remote-code.git
640	  Claude remote: /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/remote-claude.git
641	
642	Next steps — see README.md § Testing push + finalize for the full flow.
643	Quick reference with this fixture's paths:
644	  jj git push -R /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/work
645	  vc-x1 finalize --repo /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/work/.claude --squash --push main --detach
646	  vc-x1 test-fixture-rm /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8
647	```
648	
649	### Testing push + finalize
650	
651	Always test against a throwaway fixture, never the live workspace.
652	Scaffold one with `test-fixture` (above), then run the complete
653	push + finalize flow end-to-end. The code repo uses plain
654	`jj git push`; the session repo uses `vc-x1 finalize` to squash
655	trailing writes and push in one shot.
656	
657	```bash
658	base=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)
659	vc-x1 test-fixture --path "$base"
660	work="$base/work"
661	session="$base/work/.claude"
662	
663	# 1. code repo: described commit → advance main → push
664	echo hello > "$work/hello.txt"
665	jj describe @ -R "$work" -m 'feat: add hello.txt'
666	jj bookmark set main -r @ -R "$work"
667	jj git push -R "$work"
668	
669	# 2. session repo: trailing writes → finalize (squash into @-, push)
670	echo notes > "$session/notes.md"
671	vc-x1 finalize --repo "$session" --squash --push main --detach \
672	    --log "$session/finalize.log"
673	
674	# 3. inspect the detached child's log once it's done (≈10s by default)
675	sleep 12 && cat "$session/finalize.log"
676	
677	# 4. cleanup when done
678	vc-x1 test-fixture-rm "$base"
679	```
680	
681	**Why `jj git push` for code but `finalize` for `.claude`?** The
682	code repo's workflow is a plain dev commit on `@-` that we push
683	directly. The session repo mirrors the bot's runtime pattern:
684	session writes land in `@` (above the last committed dev commit),
685	and `finalize --squash @,@-` folds those trailing writes into the
686	dev commit just before pushing, so one atomic state goes upstream.
687	
688	The log file shows timestamped (nanoseconds) entries with PIDs,
689	covering the full flow: `main` entry/exit, `finalize` entry/exit,
```

```
688	The log file shows timestamped (nanoseconds) entries with PIDs,
689	covering the full flow: `main` entry/exit, `finalize` entry/exit,
690	`detach` spawn, and `finalize_exec` in the child process.
691	
692	Example — detached finalize against a fresh fixture. What the user
693	sees in the terminal (the parent process) is the pre-flight plan and
694	the detach confirmation; the child's work continues in the background:
695	```
696	$ vc-x1 finalize --repo "$session" --squash --push main \
697	    --detach --delay 1 --log "$session/finalize.log"
698	finalize: squash @ → @- in /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/work/.claude
699	finalize: set bookmark 'main' mwutluxv 31a49010 → mwutluxv 31a49010 (@-)
700	finalize: push 'main' to remote
701	finalize: detached (pid 103787), log: /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/finalize.log
702	```
703	
704	A few seconds later the log file (authoritative when the caller
705	closes the child's pipes) shows the full run, including the child's
706	own squash/push output:
707	```
708	$ cat "$session/finalize.log"
709	[INFO ] vc_x1::finalize: finalize: squash @ → @- in /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/work/.claude
710	[INFO ] vc_x1::finalize: finalize: set bookmark 'main' mwutluxv 31a49010 → mwutluxv 31a49010 (@-)
711	[INFO ] vc_x1::finalize: finalize: push 'main' to remote
712	[INFO ] vc_x1::finalize: finalize: detached (pid 103787), log: /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/finalize.log
713	[INFO ] vc_x1::common: Working copy  (@) now at: ovumtpup fa1cb861 (empty) (no description set)
714	Parent commit (@-)      : mwutluxv 584571ba main* | initial commit
715	[INFO ] vc_x1::common: Nothing changed.
716	[INFO ] vc_x1::common: Changes to push to origin:
717	  Move sideways bookmark main from 31a490102db7 to 584571ba54af
718	```
719	
720	Pre-flight failures (bookmark missing, non-tracking remote, squash
721	revset unresolved, push target lacks a description) exit the parent
722	synchronously with a non-zero status and a pointed error on stderr,
723	before the child is ever spawned.
724	
725	## Cross-repo Linking with Git Trailers
726	
727	Commits in each repo use [git trailers](https://git-scm.com/docs/git-interpret-trailers)
```

```
555	```
556	
557	`push` warns (non-fatal) when the configured state dir isn't
558	matched in `.gitignore`.
559	
560	See [Add push subcommand (0.37.0)](./notes/chores-05.md#add-push-subcommand-0370)
561	for the full design and [per-step record](./notes/chores-05.md#per-step-record)
562	for what each `0.37.0-N` dev step shipped.
563	
564	### test-fixture
565	
566	Scaffold a throwaway dual-repo jj workspace + local bare-git remotes
567	for testing `finalize` (and other subcommands) without touching live
568	workspace repos. Mirrors the real `vc-x1 init` layout minus the GitHub
569	side and the `~/.claude/projects/` symlink. Both repos get a described
570	initial commit with matching `ochid:` trailers, a tracked `main`
571	bookmark, and a pushed remote — so `finalize --push` flows work
572	end-to-end on either side.
573	
574	**Local remotes, not GitHub.** Each `origin` points at a bare-git
575	directory alongside the work trees (`<base>/remote-code.git/`,
576	`<base>/remote-claude.git/`) — no network, no auth, no GitHub. Pushes
577	succeed against these local bare repos and stay inside the fixture,
578	so nothing leaks out and nothing needs cleanup on a remote service.
579	When you're done, `vc-x1 test-fixture-rm <base>` wipes the whole
580	thing.
581	
582	```bash
583	vc-x1 test-fixture                 # base = $TMPDIR/vc-x1-test-<timestamp>
584	vc-x1 test-fixture --path /tmp/t1  # explicit path
585	vc-x1 test-fixture --use-template ../vc-template-x1  # seed from sibling templates
586	```
587	
588	`--use-template` takes the same `CODE[,BOT]` value as `vc-x1 init`
589	(bot defaults to `<CODE>.claude` sibling). Non-hidden template contents
590	are copied into `work/` and `work/.claude/`, and each repo's
591	`README.md` first line is rewritten to `# work` / `# work.claude`.
592	This is the path for eyeballing the template-copy result without
593	hitting GitHub.
594	
595	Layout:
596	```
597	<base>/
598	  remote-code.git/     bare git remote for code repo
599	  remote-claude.git/   bare git remote for .claude session repo
600	  work/                code repo (jj colocated, main tracks origin)
601	    .vc-config.toml    path="/",       other-repo=".claude"
602	    .gitignore         /.claude /.git /.jj /target /.vc-x1
603	    .claude/           session repo (jj colocated, main tracks origin)
604	      .vc-config.toml  path="/.claude", other-repo=".."
605	      .gitignore       .git .jj
606	```
607	
608	Example — running `vc-x1 test-fixture` (the timestamp suffix varies):
609	```
610	$ vc-x1 test-fixture
611	Creating test fixture at /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8
612	Step 1: Initializing bare git remotes...
613	Step 2: Initializing work repo (jj colocated)...
614	Initialized repo in "."
615	Hint: Running `git clean -xdf` will remove `.jj/`!
616	Step 3: Initializing .claude session repo (jj colocated)...
617	Initialized repo in "."
618	Hint: Running `git clean -xdf` will remove `.jj/`!
619	Step 4: Initial commits with placeholder ochids...
620	Working copy  (@) now at: znkwzomz 0ce34d9b (empty) (no description set)
621	Parent commit (@-)      : xoknyroz 93ca19eb initial commit
622	Working copy  (@) now at: kmtpuzox f8c9b5af (empty) (no description set)
623	Parent commit (@-)      : mwutluxv 40438e05 initial commit
624	Step 5: Setting ochid cross-references...
625	Rebased 1 descendant commits
626	Step 6: Setting bookmarks and wiring remotes...
627	Created 1 bookmarks pointing to xoknyroz 46b9cd88 main | initial commit
628	Created 1 bookmarks pointing to mwutluxv 31a49010 main | initial commit
629	Step 7: Pushing main to both remotes...
630	Changes to push to origin:
631	  Add bookmark main to 46b9cd886e64
632	Changes to push to origin:
633	  Add bookmark main to 31a490102db7
634	
635	Fixture ready (local bare-git remotes, see README.md § test-fixture):
636	  Code repo:     /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/work
637	  Session repo:  /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/work/.claude
638	  Code remote:   /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/remote-code.git
639	  Claude remote: /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/remote-claude.git
640	
641	Next steps — see README.md § Testing push + finalize for the full flow.
642	Quick reference with this fixture's paths:
643	  jj git push -R /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/work
644	  vc-x1 finalize --repo /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8/work/.claude --squash --push main --detach
645	  vc-x1 test-fixture-rm /tmp/vc-x1-test-8PD4x8
646	```
647	
648	### Testing push + finalize
649	
650	Always test against a throwaway fixture, never the live workspace.
651	Scaffold one with `test-fixture` (above), then run the complete
652	push + finalize flow end-to-end. The code repo uses plain
653	`jj git push`; the session repo uses `vc-x1 finalize` to squash
654	trailing writes and push in one shot.
655	
656	```bash
657	base=$(mktemp -u /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)
658	vc-x1 test-fixture --path "$base"
659	work="$base/work"
660	session="$base/work/.claude"
661	
662	# 1. code repo: described commit → advance main → push
663	echo hello > "$work/hello.txt"
664	jj describe @ -R "$work" -m 'feat: add hello.txt'
665	jj bookmark set main -r @ -R "$work"
666	jj git push -R "$work"
667	
668	# 2. session repo: trailing writes → finalize (squash into @-, push)
669	echo notes > "$session/notes.md"
670	vc-x1 finalize --repo "$session" --squash --push main --detach \
671	    --log "$session/finalize.log"
672	
673	# 3. inspect the detached child's log once it's done (≈10s by default)
674	sleep 12 && cat "$session/finalize.log"
675	
676	# 4. cleanup when done
677	vc-x1 test-fixture-rm "$base"
678	```
679	
680	**Why `jj git push` for code but `finalize` for `.claude`?** The
681	code repo's workflow is a plain dev commit on `@-` that we push
682	directly. The session repo mirrors the bot's runtime pattern:
683	session writes land in `@` (above the last committed dev commit),
684	and `finalize --squash @,@-` folds those trailing writes into the
```
